\title{The $\mathbb{Z}/9$ Vortex Framework: reflections, division-by-zero as
domain change, and a $7=6+1$ correspondence with the Clay Millennium Problems
(observation-based, recomputable)}
\author{Tsvetan Rouschev\\\small ORCID: \href{https://orcid.org/0009-0000-7312-9778}{0009-0000-7312-9778}}
\date{2026}

\newtheorem{theorem}{Theorem}[section]

\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}
We study a finite-arithmetic framework on the ring $\mathbb{Z}/9$ (the ``vortex'')
and the group $(\mathbb{Z}/7)^{*}$ (the seven-ray Pliska ``rosette''), from an
observation standpoint: each statement is computed, not asserted. Division by zero
is treated as a change of domain ($n/0 := n^{-1} \bmod 9$ on units; $0 \leftrightarrow
\infty$ on the Riemann sphere); inversion and the ten's-complement reflection
$10-d$ are involutions; a $7=6+1$ bijection maps the rosette (six units $+$ center)
onto the Clay set (six open problems $+$ Poincar\'e). A mechanical entailment test
reports \textbf{0/7}. \textbf{Observed conclusion:} the framework reflects/maps the
Millennium Problems into one algebraic structure; it does \emph{not} solve them,
and it is not a physical theory. It is a proof of concept and a classical simulator.
All results recompute from source (TypeScript; formalized in Lean~4).
\end{abstract}

\section{Scope}
This is not a resolution of the Clay Millennium Problems. The earlier
``$\exists\,\alpha,\ \top \wedge 1^2=1 \wedge 1=1$'' statements are tautologies:
true in a world where the conjecture is false, hence entailing nothing (0/7). What
is exact here is the $\mathbb{Z}/9$ / $(\mathbb{Z}/7)^{*}$ algebra, the reflections
and folds, and the geometry (cube $\to$ hexagon $\to$ Seed/Flower/Fruit/Metatron).

\section{Results (computed)}
\begin{itemize}
\item Units $(\mathbb{Z}/9)^{*}=\{1,2,4,5,7,8\}$; nilradical $\{0,3,6,9\}$ with
      $3^2\equiv 6^2\equiv 0$; doubling $\langle 2\rangle$ is $C_6$.
\item $10-d$ is an involution, fixed digit $5$; multiplicative inverses
      $2\leftrightarrow5$, $4\leftrightarrow7$.
\item $(\mathbb{Z}/7)^{*}\cong C_6 \cong (\mathbb{Z}/9)^{*}$; generator
      $3\ (\mathrm{mod}\ 7)\leftrightarrow 2\ (\mathrm{mod}\ 9)$.
\item Shell-model magic numbers $2,8,20,28,50,82,126$ (oscillator + spin-orbit);
      the $10\cdot 2^{k}$ ladder hits only the oscillator closures $20,40$.
\item Proton/electron ratio: fit $108\cdot 17 = 1836$ vs measured $1836.15267343$;
      $1836 \neq 1836.1527$ (curve-fit, not prediction).
\end{itemize}

\section{Reproducibility}
See the repository: \url{https://github.com/ceccec/millennium-solutions}. Fused
TypeScript modules recompute every result; Lean~4 theorems (\texttt{decide},
\texttt{norm\_num}) formalize the exact facts. License: CC BY-NC-ND 4.0.

\section{Theorems}

Every statement below is a declaration the Lean~4 kernel accepts, sorry-free; the exhaustions are axiom-free and the
proofs carry only the standard axioms \texttt{propext} and \texttt{Quot.sound}, and the
\LaTeX{} is generated from the Lean source and checked to read back symbol for symbol against it
(\texttt{npm run latex-gate}). There are 1,176 of them across 59 files and
7 wings; 974 close by exhaustion over a stated finite domain, 187 are proved
for every value, and the remaining 15 close by \texttt{rfl} and are counted separately, never as theorems.

\paragraph{\texttt{address.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{raw\_bytes\_of\_a}]
\[ \mathrm{rawBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) = [88,\,118,\,248,\,251,\,63,\,149,\,14,\,202,\,10,\,251,\,189,\,97,\,221,\,134,\,206,\,204] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{to\_uuid\_bytes\_of\_a}]
\[ \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) = [88,\,118,\,248,\,251,\,63,\,149,\,142,\,202,\,138,\,251,\,189,\,97,\,221,\,134,\,206,\,204] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{to\_uuid\_bytes\_of\_uuidna}]
\[ \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right) = [252,\,81,\,21,\,50,\,110,\,138,\,132,\,24,\,165,\,34,\,165,\,27,\,29,\,70,\,167,\,12] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{address\_is\_sixteen\_bytes}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right)\right| = 16 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{address\_bytes\_are\_bytes}]
\[ \forall b \in \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right),\; b < 256 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{version\_nibble\_is\_forced}]
\[ \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right)_{6}}{16} = 8 \land \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right)_{6}}{16} = 8 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{variant\_bits\_are\_forced}]
\[ \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right)_{8}}{64} = 2 \land \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right)_{8}}{64} = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addressing\_is\_deterministic}]
\[ \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) = \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{distinct\_seeds\_give\_distinct\_addresses}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addressing\_is\_injective\_on\_single\_characters}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_version\_stamp\_keeps\_the\_low\_nibble\_of\_every\_byte}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → or8 (and8 b 15) 128 = 128 + b \% 16}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_variant\_stamp\_keeps\_the\_low\_six\_bits\_of\_every\_byte}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → or8 (and8 b 63) 128 = 128 + b \% 64}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{stamping\_twice\_is\_stamping\_once}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → or8 (and8 (or8 (and8 b 15) 128) 15) 128 = or8 (and8 b 15) 128 ∧ or8 (and8 (or8 (and8 b 63) 128) 63) 128 = or8 (and8 b 63) 128}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_third\_group\_is\_version\_eight\_over\_twelve\_hash\_bits}]
\texttt{∀ b6 b7 : Nat, b6 < 256 → b7 < 256 → (or8 (and8 b6 15) 128) * 256 + b7 = 8 * 4096 + (b6 \% 16 * 256 + b7) ∧ b6 \% 16 * 256 + b7 < 4096}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fourth\_group\_is\_variant\_two\_over\_fourteen\_hash\_bits}]
\texttt{∀ b8 b9 : Nat, b8 < 256 → b9 < 256 → (or8 (and8 b8 63) 128) * 256 + b9 = 2 * 16384 + (b8 \% 64 * 256 + b9) ∧ b8 \% 64 * 256 + b9 < 16384}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unstamped\_bits\_read\_back\_from\_every\_stamped\_byte}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → and8 (or8 (and8 b 15) 128) 15 = b \% 16 ∧ and8 (or8 (and8 b 63) 128) 63 = b \% 64}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_twelve\_hash\_bits\_read\_back\_from\_the\_third\_group}]
\texttt{∀ b6 b7 : Nat, b6 < 256 → b7 < 256 → ((or8 (and8 b6 15) 128) * 256 + b7) / 4096 = 8 ∧ ((or8 (and8 b6 15) 128) * 256 + b7) \% 4096 = b6 \% 16 * 256 + b7}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fourteen\_hash\_bits\_read\_back\_from\_the\_fourth\_group}]
\texttt{∀ b8 b9 : Nat, b8 < 256 → b9 < 256 → ((or8 (and8 b8 63) 128) * 256 + b9) / 16384 = 2 ∧ ((or8 (and8 b8 63) 128) * 256 + b9) \% 16384 = b8 \% 64 * 256 + b9}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{asymmetric.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_signature\_is\_four\_uuids\_and\_a\_key\_is\_two}]
\[ \mathrm{signatureBytes} = 4 \cdot \mathrm{uuidBytes} \land \mathrm{publicKeyBytes} = 2 \cdot \mathrm{uuidBytes} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{neither\_the\_key\_nor\_the\_signature\_fits\_in\_a\_uuid}]
\[ \mathrm{uuidBytes} < \mathrm{publicKeyBytes} \land \mathrm{uuidBytes} < \mathrm{signatureBytes} \land \mathrm{uuidBytes} \cdot 8 < \mathrm{signatureBytes} \cdot 8 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{not\_even\_the\_whole\_uuid\_would\_hold\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{uuidBytes} \cdot 8 = 128 \land \mathrm{signatureBytes} \cdot 8 = 512 \land 128 < 512 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{what\_travels\_beside\_the\_identifier}]
\[ \mathrm{publicKeyBytes} + \mathrm{signatureBytes} = 96 \land 96 = 6 \cdot \mathrm{uuidBytes} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_check\_cannot\_separate\_the\_payloads}]
\[ 2^{\mathrm{checkBits}} < 2^{\mathrm{payloadBits}} \land \mathrm{payloadBits} = 90 \land \mathrm{checkBits} = 32 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gap\_is\_fifty\_eight\_bits}]
\[ \mathrm{payloadBits} - \mathrm{checkBits} = 58 \land 2^{\mathrm{payloadBits}} = 2^{58} \cdot 2^{\mathrm{checkBits}} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_checksum\_is\_the\_same\_whoever\_computes\_it}]
\[ \forall s \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall p \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{checkOf}\mathopen{}\left(s,\,p\right) = \mathrm{checkOf}\mathopen{}\left(t,\,p\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 432 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{two\_secrets\_part\_company\_on\_every\_payload}]
\[ \forall p \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,p\right) \neq \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,p\right) \land \lnot \forall s \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall p \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(s,\,p\right) = \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(t,\,p\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 5,184 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_tag\_moves\_with\_the\_payload}]
\[ \forall p \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,p\right) \neq \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,p + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_square\_root\_rule\_is\_the\_one\_this\_prime\_admits}]
\[ p \bmod 8 = 5 \land p + 3 \bmod 8 = 0 \land p \bmod 4 = 1 \land p \bmod 4 \neq 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_cofactor\_is\_bracketed\_at\_eight}]
\[ 4 \cdot L < p \land p < 8 \cdot L \land L < p \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{asymmetric\_settles\_its\_range}]
\[ \mathrm{settledHere} = 11 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{rfl} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{capacity.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_container\_is\_one\_hundred\_twenty\_eight\_and\_six\_are\_spent}]
\[ \mathrm{free} = 122 \land \mathrm{reserved} = 6 \land \mathrm{free} + \mathrm{reserved} = \mathrm{container} \land \mathrm{container} = 128 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_capacity\_is\_two\_to\_the\_free\_bits\_and\_not\_the\_container}]
\[ 2^{\mathrm{free}} = 5316911983139663491615228241121378304 \land 2^{\mathrm{container}} = 340282366920938463463374607431768211456 \land 2^{\mathrm{free}} < 2^{\mathrm{container}} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nominal\_overstates\_the\_real\_by\_two\_to\_the\_reserved}]
\[ \frac{2^{\mathrm{container}}}{2^{\mathrm{free}}} = 2^{\mathrm{reserved}} \land 2^{\mathrm{reserved}} = 64 \land 2^{\mathrm{container}} = 64 \cdot 2^{\mathrm{free}} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_collision\_exponent\_follows\_the\_free\_bits}]
\[ \frac{\mathrm{free}}{2} = 61 \land \frac{\mathrm{container}}{2} = 64 \land 2^{\frac{\mathrm{free}}{2}} = 2305843009213693952 \land 2^{\frac{\mathrm{container}}{2}} = 18446744073709551616 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_birthday\_gap\_is\_half\_the\_reservation}]
\[ \frac{2^{\frac{\mathrm{container}}{2}}}{2^{\frac{\mathrm{free}}{2}}} = 8 \land 8 = 2^{\frac{\mathrm{reserved}}{2}} \land \frac{\mathrm{reserved}}{2} = 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gap\_is\_zero\_exactly\_when\_nothing\_is\_reserved}]
\[ \forall r \in \{0,\dots,32\},\; \frac{2^{128}}{2^{128 - r}} = 2^{r} \land r = 0 = \frac{2^{128}}{2^{128 - r}} = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 33 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_overclaim\_is\_this\_many\_addresses\_that\_do\_not\_exist}]
\[ 2^{\mathrm{container}} - 2^{\mathrm{free}} = 334965454937798799971759379190646833152 \land 2^{\mathrm{container}} - 2^{\mathrm{free}} = 63 \cdot 2^{\mathrm{free}} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_bound\_is\_a\_ceiling\_on\_difficulty\_and\_not\_a\_measurement}]
\[ 2^{\frac{\mathrm{free}}{2}} < 2^{\mathrm{free}} \land 2^{\frac{\mathrm{free}}{2}} < 2^{\frac{\mathrm{container}}{2}} \land \frac{\mathrm{free}}{2} < \mathrm{free} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 160 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_low\_bit\_sees\_what\_the\_residue\_cannot}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_two\_by\_seven\_reads\_more\_than\_the\_seven}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{four\_digits\_stay\_unread\_and\_are\_counted}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_wrap\_is\_what\_makes\_it\_a\_torus}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_distance\_is\_symmetric\_and\_zero\_only\_on\_itself}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{d1}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{d1}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \land \mathrm{d1}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = 0 = a = b \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_triangle\_inequality\_holds\_on\_every\_triple}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{decide}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{d1}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,c\right) \le \mathrm{d1}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) + \mathrm{d1}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,c\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}

\paragraph{\texttt{authority.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nothing\_withdrawn\_is\_live}]
\[ \forall k \in \mathrm{withdrawn},\; \lnot k \in \mathrm{live} \land \forall k \in \mathrm{live},\; \lnot k \in \mathrm{withdrawn} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_sealed\_statement\_is\_live\_or\_withdrawn\_and\_not\_both}]
\[ \forall k \in \mathrm{sealed},\; k \in \mathrm{live} \lor k \in \mathrm{withdrawn} \land \lnot k \in \mathrm{live} \land k \in \mathrm{withdrawn} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{withdrawing\_shrinks\_the\_live\_set\_and\_never\_the\_record}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{live}\right| + \left|\mathrm{withdrawn}\right| = \left|\mathrm{sealed}\right| \land \forall k \in \mathrm{withdrawn},\; k \in \mathrm{sealed} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_rider\_and\_a\_verdict\_are\_different\_things}]
\[ \exists k \in \mathrm{carriesRider} : \lnot k \in \mathrm{isVerdict} \land \exists k \in \mathrm{isVerdict} : \lnot k \in \mathrm{carriesRider} \land \exists k \in \mathrm{carriesRider} : k \in \mathrm{isVerdict} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{withdrawing\_never\_raises\_the\_frozen\_count}]
\texttt{(List.range 256).all (fun m => let taken := bits m let after := carriesRider.filter (fun k => ! taken.contains k) after.length ≤ carriesRider.length)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{clearing\_changes\_no\_seal}]
\[ \forall k \in \mathrm{cleared},\; k \in \mathrm{sealed} \land \left|\{\, k \in \mathrm{sealed} \mid \lnot k \in \mathrm{cleared} \,\}\right| + \left|\mathrm{cleared}\right| = \left|\mathrm{sealed}\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_cleared\_statement\_leaves\_the\_queue\_and\_stays\_on\_the\_record}]
\[ \forall k \in \{\, k \in \mathrm{isVerdict} \mid \lnot k \in \mathrm{cleared} \,\},\; k \in \mathrm{sealed} \land \left|\{\, k \in \mathrm{isVerdict} \mid \lnot k \in \mathrm{cleared} \,\}\right| \le \left|\mathrm{isVerdict}\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{refusing\_every\_verdict\_never\_refuses\_the\_whole\_record}]
\[ \forall k \in \mathrm{isVerdict},\; k \in \mathrm{sealed} \land \exists k \in \mathrm{sealed} : \lnot k \in \mathrm{isVerdict} \land \left|\mathrm{isVerdict}\right| < \left|\mathrm{sealed}\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{chi\_is\_injective\_for\_every\_g\_h}]
\texttt{∀ g h : Nat, chi g = chi h → g = h}

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\texttt{∀ n k : Nat, repunit (n + k) / 10 ^ k = repunit n ∧ repunit (n + k) \% 10 ^ k = repunit k}

\paragraph{\texttt{energy.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{any\_yield\_below\_its\_cost\_reads\_under\_the\_whole}]
\[ \forall c \in \{1,\dots,60\},\; \forall y \in \{0,\dots,c-1\},\; \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,c\right) < 100 \land \mathrm{burnYield} < \mathrm{splitCost} \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_chain\_of\_efficiencies\_can\_only\_lose}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,100\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,100\},\; a \cdot b \le 100 \cdot a \land a \cdot b \le 100 \cdot b \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,201 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_cost\_per\_litre\_multiplies\_back\_to\_the\_cost}]
\[ \forall l \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{l} \cdot l \le \mathrm{splitCost} \land \mathrm{splitCost} < \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{l} + 1 \cdot l \land \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{\mathrm{waterOut}} = 5777 \land \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{\mathrm{waterOut}} > \mathrm{roPerLitre} \cdot 1000 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_two\_to\_one\_is\_forced\_by\_the\_oxygen}]
\[ \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,2,\,1\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,1,\,1\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \left|\bigcup_{a \in \{1,\dots,9\}} \bigcup_{b \in \{1,\dots,9\}} \{\, c \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \,\}\right| = 4 \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,4,\,2\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(6,\,6,\,3\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(8,\,8,\,4\right) = \mathrm{true} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mass\_balance\_is\_the\_atom\_balance}]
\[ \mathrm{mgH2} = \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,0\right) \land \mathrm{mgO2} = \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,2\right) \land \mathrm{mgH2O} = \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,1\right) \land \forall a \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall c \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; a \cdot \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,1\right) = b \cdot \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,0\right) + c \cdot \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,2\right) = \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \land 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} = 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} + \mathrm{mgO2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_atom\_table\_decides\_the\_balance}]
\[ \forall a \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall c \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; a \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2O}_{1} = b \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2}_{1} + c \cdot \mathrm{atomsO2}_{1} \land a \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2O}_{2} = b \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2}_{2} + c \cdot \mathrm{atomsO2}_{2} = \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gases\_are\_two\_to\_one\_and\_consume\_each\_other\_exactly}]
\[ 2 \cdot 1 = 2 \land \frac{2 \cdot 100}{3} = 66 \land \frac{1 \cdot 100}{3} = 33 \land \lnot 1 \cdot 1 = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_part\_and\_its\_rest\_read\_back\_the\_whole}]
\[ \forall w \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall a \in \{0,\dots,w + 1-1\},\; \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,w\right) + \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(w - a,\,w\right) \le 10000 \land 10000 \le \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,w\right) + \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(w - a,\,w\right) + 1 \land \frac{\mathrm{mgO2} \cdot 100}{2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2}} = 793 \land \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2},\,2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = 1119 \land \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mgO2},\,2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = 8880 \land 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} - 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} = \mathrm{mgO2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{mass\_is\_conserved\_at\_every\_scale\_so\_the\_loop\_cannot\_make\_water}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,100\},\; 2 \cdot n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} = 2 \cdot n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} + n \cdot \mathrm{mgO2} \land \lnot \forall n \in \{1,\dots,100\},\; n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} = n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} + n \cdot \mathrm{mgO2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{one\_litre\_split\_returns\_one\_litre\_burnt}]
\[ 111900 + 888100 = 1000000 \land \frac{888100 \cdot 100}{111900} = 793 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{only\_oxy\_hydrogen\_burns\_without\_admitting\_nitrogen}]
\[ \frac{7808 \cdot 100}{2095} = 372 \land 0 \cdot 372 = 0 \land \lnot 372 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{splitting\_then\_burning\_is\_the\_identity\_at\_the\_ideal}]
\[ \forall e \in \{0,\dots,999\},\; \mathrm{burnE}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{splitE}\mathopen{}\left(e\right)\right) = e \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{splitting\_then\_burning\_is\_the\_identity\_at\_every\_ledger\_value}]
\texttt{∀ e : Nat, burnE (splitE e) = e}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gas\_volume\_reads\_back\_its\_moles}]
\[ \forall m \in \{0,\dots,999\},\; \mathrm{molesOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(m\right)\right) \le m \land m \le \mathrm{molesOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(m\right)\right) + 1 \land \mathrm{molH2} = 2 \cdot \mathrm{molO2} \land \mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2}\right) = 11117 \land \mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molO2}\right) = 5558 \land \frac{\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2}\right) + \mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molO2}\right)}{9} = 1852 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{two\_parts\_in\_three\_is\_two\_thirds\_at\_every\_scale}]
\[ \forall m \in \{1,\dots,500\},\; \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot m,\,3 \cdot m\right) = 66 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2},\,\mathrm{molH2} + \mathrm{molO2}\right) = 66 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2},\,2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = 11 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 500 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_energy\_per\_litre\_multiplies\_back\_to\_the\_energy}]
\[ \forall l \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \frac{\mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100}{l} \cdot l \le \mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100 \land \mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100 < \frac{\mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100}{l} + 1 \cdot l \land \frac{\mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100}{\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2}\right)} = 299 \land \frac{\mathrm{petrolWhL} \cdot 100}{299} = 3244 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{compression\_scales\_the\_energy\_per\_litre\_linearly}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; 2 \cdot \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) \le \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot k\right) \land \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot k\right) \le 2 \cdot \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) + 1 \land \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(42\right) = 1398 \land \frac{\mathrm{petrolWhL}}{\mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(42\right)} = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 100 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_ratio\_and\_its\_reciprocal\_multiply\_back\_to\_the\_whole}]
\[ \forall b \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall a \in \{1,\dots,1+b-1\},\; \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \cdot \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \le 10000 \land 10000 < \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) + 1 \cdot \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) + 1 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{splitCost},\,\mathrm{burnYield}\right) = 433 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \land \frac{9 \cdot 1000}{\frac{\mathrm{burnYield}}{1000}} = 750 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_loss\_and\_the\_yield\_read\_back\_the\_input}]
\[ \forall c \in \{1,\dots,60\},\; \forall y \in \{0,\dots,c + 1-1\},\; c - y + y = c \land 99 \le \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(c - y,\,c\right) + \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,c\right) \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(c - y,\,c\right) + \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,c\right) \le 100 \land \mathrm{splitCost} - \mathrm{burnYield} = 40000 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{splitCost} - \mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 76 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_residue\_ratio\_is\_the\_same\_at\_every\_volume}]
\[ \forall l \in \{1,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{residueMg}\mathopen{}\left(l,\,\mathrm{tdsSeawater}\right) = 700 \cdot \mathrm{residueMg}\mathopen{}\left(l,\,\mathrm{tdsTapWater}\right) \land \frac{\mathrm{residueMg}\mathopen{}\left(100,\,\mathrm{tdsSeawater}\right)}{1000} = 3500 \land \frac{\mathrm{residueMg}\mathopen{}\left(100,\,\mathrm{tdsTapWater}\right)}{1000} = 5 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_atoms\_read\_back\_from\_the\_mass}]
\[ \forall h \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; \forall o \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; \mathrm{atomsOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(h,\,o\right)\right) = \mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(\left(h,\,o\right)\right) \land \mathrm{atomsOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mgH2}\right) = \mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(\left(2,\,0\right)\right) \land \mathrm{atomsOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mgO2}\right) = \mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(\left(0,\,2\right)\right) \land \mathrm{atomsOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = \mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(\left(2,\,1\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 100 cases. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{handle.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_four\_hex\_field\_round\_trips\_over\_its\_whole\_space}]
\texttt{(List.range 16).all (fun a => (List.range 16).all (fun b => (List.range 16).all (fun c => (List.range 16).all (fun d => let n := fromNibs a b c d nib n 0 == a \&\& nib n 1 == b \&\& nib n 2 == c \&\& nib n 3 == d \&\& n < 65536))))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 65,536 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_field\_runs\_from\_zero\_to\_its\_last\_value}]
\[ \mathrm{fromNibs}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,0,\,0,\,0\right) = 0 \land \mathrm{fromNibs}\mathopen{}\left(15,\,15,\,15,\,15\right) = 65535 \land \forall d \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{fromNibs}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,0,\,0,\,d\right) = 4096 \cdot d \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_uuid\_is\_thirty\_two\_hex\_and\_exactly\_three\_groups\_are\_four\_wide}]
\[ \sum \mathrm{groups} = 32 \land \left|\{\, g \in \mathrm{groups} \mid g = 4 \,\}\right| = 3 \land \left|\mathrm{groups}\right| = 5 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_address\_space\_is\_finite\_so\_enough\_messages\_must\_share\_an\_address}]
\[ \mathrm{space} = 16^{4} \land \mathrm{space} = 2^{16} \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, m \bmod 16 \mid m \in \{0,\dots,16\} \,\}\right)\right| < 17 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 17 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{one\_address\_two\_messages\_and\_the\_handle\_is\_what\_tells\_them\_apart}]
\[ \mathrm{addr}\mathopen{}\left(3\right) = \mathrm{addr}\mathopen{}\left(19\right) \land \mathrm{under}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,3\right) = \mathrm{under}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,19\right) \land \mathrm{under}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,3\right) \neq \mathrm{under}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,19\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_same\_message\_at\_two\_positions\_never\_seals\_alike}]
\[ \forall m \in \{0,\dots,31\},\; \forall i \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \forall j \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; i = j \lor \mathrm{sealAt}\mathopen{}\left(m,\,i\right) \neq \mathrm{sealAt}\mathopen{}\left(m,\,j\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 2,048 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reordering\_the\_handles\_always\_moves\_the\_fifth}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{fold4}\mathopen{}\left(p_{0},\,p_{1},\,p_{2},\,p_{3}\right) \mid p \in \mathrm{perms} \,\}\right)\right| = 24 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_address\_never\_determines\_the\_message\_it\_addresses}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; 1 < \left|\{\, m \in \{0,\dots,63\} \mid \mathrm{addr}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) = a \,\}\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,024 cases. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{index.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_tens\_complement\_is\_an\_involution\_with\_one\_fixed\_point}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \land \left|\{\, d \in \{0,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \,\}\right| = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 100 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{each\_unit\_has\_exactly\_one\_inverse\_and\_each\_non\_unit\_none}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \left|\{\, e \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid d \cdot e \bmod 9 = 1 \,\}\right| = \begin{cases}1 & \text{if } \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\\ 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_doubling\_orbit\_stays\_in\_the\_ring\_for\_forty\_eight\_steps}]
\[ \forall v \in \mathrm{orbit}[\{0,\dots,47\}],\; v < 9 \land \forall k \in \{0,\dots,47\},\; \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) \in \mathrm{span} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 2,304 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_doubling\_orbit\_first\_returns\_to\_one\_at\_six}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; k = 0 \lor \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) \neq 1 \land \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_span\_is\_exactly\_the\_units\_of\_the\_ring}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; d \in \mathrm{span} = \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \land \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \lor \lnot d \in \mathrm{span} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_span\_and\_the\_units\_both\_sum\_to\_zero\_mod\_nine}]
\[ \sum \mathrm{span} \bmod 9 = 0 \land \sum \{\, x \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\} \bmod 9 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_orbit\_is\_one\_closed\_loop\_of\_six\_distinct\_points}]
\[ \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(0\right) \land \forall i \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall j \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(i\right) = \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(j\right) = i = j \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 36 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_seven\_rest\_on\_one\_finite\_structure}]
\[ \forall d \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \land \left|\{\, x \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| = 6 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{span}\right)\right| = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_three\_non\_units\_are\_exactly\_the\_unreachable}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \lnot \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \,\} = [0,\,3,\,6] \land \forall d \in \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \lnot \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \,\},\; \lnot d \in \mathrm{span} \land \left|\{\, x \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| + \left|\{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \lnot \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \,\}\right| = 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 19,683 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_origin\_annihilates\_and\_never\_joins\_the\_circuit}]
\[ \forall e \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; 0 \cdot e \bmod 9 \neq 1 \land \lnot 0 \in \mathrm{span} \land \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; d + 0 \bmod 9 = d \bmod 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_sequence\_is\_its\_named\_parts\_and\_closes}]
\[ \mathrm{sequence} = \mathrm{span} \mathbin{+\!\!+} [3,\,6,\,9] \mathbin{+\!\!+} [0] \mathbin{+\!\!+} [1] \land \left|\mathrm{sequence}\right| = 11 \land \operatorname{head}\left(\mathrm{sequence}\right) = \operatorname{last}\left(\mathrm{sequence}\right) \land 9 \bmod 9 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 3 cases. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{instruments.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_strict\_order\_is\_what\_is\_admitted}]
\[ \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(4\right),\,\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(5\right)\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(5\right),\,\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(5\right)\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(6\right),\,\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(5\right)\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{admission\_is\_exactly\_the\_strict\_order}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(b\right)\right) = \mathrm{decide}\mathopen{}\left(a < b\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 144 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_unknown\_instant\_is\_never\_admitted}]
\[ \forall t \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{none},\,\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(t\right)\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{some}\mathopen{}\left(t\right),\,\mathrm{none}\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \mathrm{precedes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{none},\,\mathrm{none}\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_boundary\_refuses\_more\_than\_it\_admits}]
\texttt{let cases := (List.range 8).map (fun a => precedes (some a) (some 4)) cases.count true = 4 ∧ cases.count false = 4}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_hole\_is\_an\_absence\_with\_a\_later\_presence}]
\[ \mathrm{holes}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true}]\right) = [1] \land \mathrm{tail}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true}]\right) = [] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_stale\_tail\_is\_an\_absence\_with\_nothing\_after\_it}]
\[ \mathrm{tail}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = [2,\,3] \land \mathrm{holes}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = [] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 2 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_two\_causes\_occur\_together\_and\_stay\_apart}]
\[ \mathrm{holes}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = [1] \land \mathrm{tail}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = [3] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nothing\_built\_is\_all\_tail\_and\_no\_hole}]
\[ \mathrm{holes}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = [] \land \mathrm{tail}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = [0,\,1,\,2] \land \mathrm{lastPresent}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{false}]\right) = \mathrm{none} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 3 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{everything\_built\_accuses\_nobody}]
\[ \mathrm{holes}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true}]\right) = [] \land \mathrm{tail}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true}]\right) = [] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pattern\_generator\_is\_complete}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{bits}\mathopen{}\left(5\right)\right| = 32 \land \left|\mathrm{bits}\mathopen{}\left(3\right)\right| = 8 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{bits}\mathopen{}\left(3\right)\right)\right| = 8 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_absence\_is\_charged\_to\_exactly\_one\_cause}]
\texttt{((bits 1) ++ (bits 2) ++ (bits 3) ++ (bits 4) ++ (bits 5)).all (fun l => let h := holes l let t := tail l let missing := (idx l).filterMap (fun p => if !p.2 then some p.1 else none) (h.all (fun x => !(t.contains x))) \&\& ((h ++ t).length == missing.length) \&\& missing.all (fun m => (h ++ t).contains m))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_echo\_carries\_the\_signal\_only\_until\_it\_is\_subtracted}]
\[ \mathrm{contains}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{echoed},\,\mathrm{SITE}\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{contains}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{echoed}\right),\,\mathrm{SITE}\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_page\_s\_own\_words\_survive\_the\_subtraction}]
\[ \mathrm{contains}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{genuine},\,\mathrm{SITE}\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{contains}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{genuine}\right),\,\mathrm{SITE}\right) = \mathrm{true} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_copy\_of\_the\_witness\_is\_removed}]
\[ \mathrm{contains}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{twice}\right),\,\mathrm{SITE}\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{subtracting\_twice\_is\_subtracting\_once}]
\[ \mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{echoed}\right)\right) = \mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{echoed}\right) \land \mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{genuine}\right)\right) = \mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{genuine}\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_empty\_witness\_removes\_nothing}]
\[ \mathrm{unreflect}\mathopen{}\left(\left|\mathrm{genuine}\right|,\,[],\,\mathrm{genuine}\right) = \mathrm{genuine} \land \mathrm{unreflect}\mathopen{}\left(\left|\mathrm{echoed}\right|,\,[],\,\mathrm{echoed}\right) = \mathrm{echoed} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{text\_without\_the\_witness\_is\_untouched}]
\[ \mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{SITE}\right) = \mathrm{SITE} \land \mathrm{clip}\mathopen{}\left([60,\,112,\,62]\right) = [60,\,112,\,62] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{any\_negator\_before\_the\_claim\_refuses\_it}]
\[ \mathrm{refused}\mathopen{}\left([7,\,2,\,9],\,5\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{refused}\mathopen{}\left([7,\,9],\,5\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \mathrm{refused}\mathopen{}\left([5],\,5\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_window\_is\_monotone\_in\_the\_claim\_s\_position}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; \forall d \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; \begin{cases}\lnot \mathrm{refused}\mathopen{}\left([3],\,c\right) \lor \mathrm{refused}\mathopen{}\left([3],\,d\right) & \text{if } c \le d\\ \mathrm{true} & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 100 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{with\_no\_negator\_nothing\_is\_cleared}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; \mathrm{refused}\mathopen{}\left([],\,c\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_path\_dirty\_before\_the\_run\_is\_never\_attributed\_to\_it}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall p \in \mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(t\right)\right),\; \lnot p \in \mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_untracked\_path\_is\_never\_attributed}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall p \in \mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(t\right)\right),\; p \in \mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(t\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nothing\_is\_attributed\_that\_is\_not\_dirty\_afterwards}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{subset}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(t\right)\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_wider\_record\_of\_what\_was\_dirty\_attributes\_no\_more}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall \mathrm{a'} \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \lnot \mathrm{subset}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{a'}\right)\right) \lor \mathrm{subset}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{a'}\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right),\,\mathrm{paths}\right),\,\mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right),\,\mathrm{paths}\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_change\_made\_by\_another\_hand\_during\_the\_run\_is\_charged\_to\_the\_run}]
\[ \exists a \in \{0,\dots,15\} : \exists b \in \{0,\dots,15\} : \exists p \in \mathrm{paths} : \lnot p \in \mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right) \land p \in \mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right) \land p \in \mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(b\right),\,\mathrm{paths}\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_run\_that\_changes\_nothing\_attributes\_nothing}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{mine}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(a\right),\,\mathrm{setOf}\mathopen{}\left(t\right)\right) = [] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reverting\_once\_leaves\_nothing\_further\_to\_attribute}]
\texttt{(List.range 16).all (fun a => (List.range 16).all (fun b => (List.range 16).all (fun t => let m := mine (setOf a) (setOf b) (setOf t) mine (setOf a) ((setOf b).filter (fun p => ! m.contains p)) (setOf t) == [])))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_reverted\_and\_the\_untouched\_partition\_what\_is\_dirty}]
\texttt{(List.range 16).all (fun a => (List.range 16).all (fun b => let m := mine (setOf a) (setOf b) paths let rest := (setOf b).filter (fun p => ! m.contains p) m.all (fun p => ! rest.contains p) \&\& (setOf b).all (fun p => m.contains p || rest.contains p)))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{instruments\_settles\_its\_range}]

\paragraph{\texttt{lanes.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_lane\_is\_always\_granted}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall r \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,r\right) \ge 1 \land \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \ge 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{lanes\_never\_exceed\_the\_cores\_but\_for\_the\_floor}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall r \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,r\right) \le \mathrm{max}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,c\right) \land \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \le \mathrm{max}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,c\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{lanes\_never\_exceed\_what\_memory\_affords}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall r \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,r\right) \le \mathrm{max}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,m\right) \land \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \le \mathrm{max}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,m\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{unknown\_is\_never\_better\_than\_measuring\_an\_empty\_machine}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \le \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,0\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{more\_memory\_never\_costs\_a\_lane}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \forall r \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,r\right) \le \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m + 1,\,r\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 512 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{more\_neighbours\_never\_buys\_a\_lane}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall r \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,r + 1\right) \le \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m,\,r\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 648 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fallback\_costs\_half\_the\_lanes\_at\_the\_size\_that\_prompted\_this}]
\[ \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,2,\,0\right) = 2 \land \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,4,\,0\right) = 4 \land \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,2,\,0\right) \cdot 2 = \mathrm{lanesKnown}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,4,\,0\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_unmeasurable\_host\_still\_builds\_and\_still\_fits}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \ge 1 \land \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \le \mathrm{max}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,m\right) \land \mathrm{lanesUnknown}\mathopen{}\left(c,\,m\right) \le \mathrm{max}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,c\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_on\_the\_triad\_and\_four\_on\_the\_units}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_seven\_roots\_miss\_four\_residues}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_absent\_residues\_are\_the\_primes\_below\_nine}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{and\_a\_change\_of\_unit\_destroys\_it}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{doubling\_the\_domain\_leaves\_the\_same\_hole}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_successor\_leaves\_every\_domain\_and\_the\_predecessor\_returns}]
\[ \forall n \in \mathrm{bounds} \mathbin{+\!\!+} [\mathrm{largestDomainHere}],\; n + 1 > n \land n + 1 - 1 = n \land n + 1 \bmod 2 \neq n \bmod 2 \]

  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{roots.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_initial\_value\_is\_that\_square\_root\_fraction}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{speed.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_verify\_path\_is\_the\_exponent}]
\[ \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,1024\right) = 10 \land \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,16384\right) = 14 \land \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,262144\right) = 18 \land \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,1048576\right) = 20 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gap\_widens\_with\_every\_doubling}]
\[ \frac{1024}{10} < \frac{16384}{14} \land \frac{16384}{14} < \frac{262144}{18} \land \frac{262144}{18} < \frac{1048576}{20} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{no\_constant\_factor\_accounts\_for\_the\_gap}]
\[ \lnot 10000 \cdot 20 \ge 1048576 \land 100 \cdot 10 \ge 1024 - 24 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_measured\_ratio\_exceeds\_the\_counted\_gap}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \frac{2^{k}}{\mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,2^{k}\right)} \le \frac{\mathrm{recomputeUs}}{\mathrm{verifyUs}} \land \frac{\mathrm{recomputeUs}}{\mathrm{verifyUs}} \cdot \mathrm{verifyUs} \le \mathrm{recomputeUs} \land \mathrm{recomputeUs} < \frac{\mathrm{recomputeUs}}{\mathrm{verifyUs}} + 1 \cdot \mathrm{verifyUs} \land \frac{\mathrm{recomputeUs}}{\mathrm{verifyUs}} = 567971 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{verification\_grows\_it\_is\_not\_constant}]
\[ \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,1024\right) < \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,1048576\right) \land \forall k \in \{1,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{rounds}\mathopen{}\left(40,\,2^{k}\right) = k \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_microseconds\_return\_from\_the\_nanoseconds\_at\_every\_count}]
\texttt{∀ u : Nat, u * 1000 / 1000 = u}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_verify\_in\_nanoseconds\_reads\_back\_its\_microseconds}]
\[ \forall u \in \{0,\dots,999\},\; \frac{u \cdot 1000}{1000} = u \land \mathrm{nsPerVerify} = \mathrm{verifyUs} \cdot 1000 \land \frac{\mathrm{nsPerVerify}}{1000} = \mathrm{verifyUs} \land \mathrm{nsPerVerify} > 10000 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_advantage\_is\_the\_count\_not\_the\_operation}]
\[ 1048576 - 20 = 1048556 \land \frac{1048576}{20} = 52428 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_break\_even\_is\_the\_ratio\_of\_verifications}]
\[ \frac{1048576}{20} = 52428 \land 52428 \cdot 20 \le 1048576 \land 1048576 < 52429 \cdot 20 + 20 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_width\_is\_the\_ceiling\_of\_128\_bits\_over\_the\_symbol}]
\[ \forall b \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(b\right) \cdot b \ge 128 \land \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(b\right) - 1 \cdot b < 128 \land \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) = \mathrm{hexChars} \land \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = \mathrm{hexbitChars} \land \mathrm{hexbitChars} < \mathrm{hexChars} \land \frac{\mathrm{hexbitChars} \cdot 100}{\mathrm{hexChars}} = 68 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{hexbits\_are\_slower\_and\_the\_ratio\_multiplies\_back}]
\[ \forall b \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \forall a \in \{0,\dots,399\},\; \frac{a}{b} \cdot b \le a \land a < \frac{a}{b} + 1 \cdot b \land \mathrm{hexbitMs} > \mathrm{hexMs} \land \frac{\mathrm{hexbitMs} \cdot 10}{\mathrm{hexMs}} = 18 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nanoseconds\_return\_from\_the\_microseconds\_at\_every\_whole\_count}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n \% 1000 = 0 → n / 1000 * 1000 = n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{theology.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mirror\_and\_the\_doubling\_agree\_at\_no\_residue}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) \bmod 9 \neq 2 \cdot \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \bmod 9 \land \left|\{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) \bmod 9 = 2 \cdot \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \bmod 9 \,\}\right| = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{confirming\_a\_witness\_is\_uniform\_while\_finding\_one\_is\_not}]
\[ \forall d \in \{\, x \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; d \cdot \mathrm{invOf}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \bmod 9 = 1 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{searchCost}[\{\, x \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}]\right)\right| > 1 \land \exists d \in \{\, x \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\} : \mathrm{searchCost}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) > \mathrm{searchCost}\mathopen{}\left(1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{no\_start\_ever\_crosses\_between\_the\_units\_and\_the\_non\_units}]
\[ \forall s \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall k \in \{0,\dots,47\},\; \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{iter}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,s\right)\right) = \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 432 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_origin\_is\_reached\_at\_every\_step\_exactly\_by\_the\_origin}]
\[ \forall s \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall k \in \{0,\dots,47\},\; \mathrm{iter}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,s\right) = 0 = s = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 432 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{generating\_the\_same\_set\_is\_exactly\_being\_of\_the\_same\_kind}]
\[ \forall g \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \forall h \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{sameSet}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{spanOf}\mathopen{}\left(g\right),\,\mathrm{spanOf}\mathopen{}\left(h\right)\right) = \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(g\right) = \mathrm{isUnit}\mathopen{}\left(h\right) \land g = 0 = h = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_orbit\_is\_antipodal\_and\_each\_opposite\_pair\_sums\_to\_nine}]
\[ \forall i \in \{0,\dots,2\},\; \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(i\right) + \mathrm{orbit}\mathopen{}\left(i + 3\right) = 9 \land \sum \mathrm{span} \bmod 9 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 3 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_orbit\_has\_no\_doubling\_closed\_part\_but\_nothing\_and\_itself}]
\texttt{(List.range 64).all (fun m => let s := subsetOf m (! s.all (fun v => s.contains (dbl v))) || s.length == 0 || s.length == span.length) ∧ span.all (fun v => span.contains (dbl v))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 64 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pairing\_is\_one\_ordering\_of\_seven\_out\_of\_all\_of\_them}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{pairing}\right)\right| = 7 \land \forall i \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; i \in \mathrm{pairing} \land \left|\mathrm{pairing}\right| = \left|\{0,\dots,6\}\right| \land \mathrm{fact}\mathopen{}\left(\left|\mathrm{pairing}\right|\right) = 5040 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 49 cases. \hfill$\square$

\subsection{the ring}

\paragraph{\texttt{closure.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nine\_names\_denote\_seven\_distinct\_properties}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{named}\right| = 9 \land \left|\operatorname{fold}_{\mathrm{acc},\,t \mapsto \begin{cases}\mathrm{acc} & \text{if } t \in \mathrm{acc}\\ \mathrm{acc} \mathbin{+\!\!+} [t] & \text{otherwise}\end{cases}}\left(\mathrm{table}[\mathrm{named}],\, \left([] : \mathrm{List}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{List}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{Bool}\right)\right)\right)\right)\right| = 7 \land \mathrm{table}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{isUnit}\right) = \mathrm{table}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{inSpan}\right) \land \mathrm{table}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{isOrigin}\right) = \mathrm{table}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{isFixed}\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_witness\_disagrees\_with\_property\_i\_at\_residue\_i}]
\texttt{(List.range 9).all (fun i => unnamed i != (named.getD i (fun \_ => false)) i)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_witness\_is\_none\_of\_the\_nine\_named\_properties}]
\texttt{(List.range 9).all (fun i => table unnamed != table (named.getD i (fun \_ => false)))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_witness\_is\_this\_table}]
\[ \mathrm{table}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unnamed}\right) = [\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{true},\,\mathrm{false}] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_property\_that\_is\_named\_is\_found\_among\_the\_named}]
\texttt{(List.range 9).any (fun i => table isUnit == table (named.getD i (fun \_ => false))) \&\& table isUnit == table (named.getD 0 (fun \_ => false)) \&\& table isUnit != table unnamed}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_diagonal\_escapes\_nine\_and\_stops}]
\texttt{let ten := named ++ [unnamed] let next := fun d => !((ten.getD d (fun \_ => false)) d) ten.length == 10 \&\& (List.range 9).all (fun i => table next != table (named.getD i (fun \_ => false))) \&\& table next == table unnamed \&\& table next == table (ten.getD 9 (fun \_ => false))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nine\_residues\_carry\_five\_hundred\_and\_twelve\_properties}]
\[ 2^{9} = 512 \land \mathrm{R.length} = 9 \land \left|\mathrm{named}\right| = 9 \land 512 - 7 = 505 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_witness\_is\_decided\_here\_and\_is\_no\_kind\_of\_limit}]
\texttt{R.all (fun d => unnamed d == !((named.getD d (fun \_ => false)) d)) \&\& (table unnamed).length == 9 \&\& ((table unnamed).filter (fun b => b)).length == 7}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{coils.lean}}

x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \forall x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8],\; x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \,\} \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(4 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\},\; x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \forall x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8],\; x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(4 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(4 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(4 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \,\},\; x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \forall x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8],\; x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(9 - d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(9 - d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(9 - d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \,\},\; x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \forall x \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8],\; x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(5 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(5 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(5 \cdot d\right) \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \,\},\; \begin{theorem}[\texttt{exactly\_one\_digit\_reflects\_out\_of\_range}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_classes\_partition\_the\_nine}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_digit\_is\_sorted\_exactly\_once}]

\end{theorem}

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,100,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{eight\_and\_eight\_make\_sixteen\_which\_reflects\_to\_ninety\_four}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{eight\_times\_eight\_is\_the\_pair\_six\_four\_read\_both\_ways}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_pair\_written\_as\_two\_digits\_is\_one\_mod\_nine}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 5 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reflecting\_both\_digits\_twice\_returns\_the\_number}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{exactly\_one\_two\_digit\_number\_reflects\_past\_its\_width\_and\_it\_is\_ten}]
\texttt{refl 0 == 10 \&\& refl2 10 == 100 \&\& refl2 (refl2 10) == 10 \&\& ((List.range 100).drop 10).filter (fun n => refl2 n > 99) == [10] \&\& ((List.range 100).drop 10).all (fun n => (refl2 n > 99) == (n == refl 0))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nines\_complement\_gives\_no\_such\_constant}]
\texttt{((List.range 100).drop 10).any (fun n => n + ((9 - n / 10) * 10 + (9 - n \% 10)) != 110) \&\& ((List.range 100).drop 10).all (fun n => n + ((9 - n / 10) * 10 + (9 - n \% 10)) == 99) \&\& 99 != 110}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{each\_triple\_produces\_the\_dimension\_it\_claims}]
\[ \left(\mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right),\,\mathrm{lengthOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right),\,\mathrm{timeOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right)\right) = \left(0,\,2,\,0\right) \land \left(\mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-5,\,1\right),\,\mathrm{lengthOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-5,\,1\right),\,\mathrm{timeOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-5,\,1\right)\right) = \left(0,\,0,\,2\right) \land \left(\mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,1,\,-1\right),\,\mathrm{lengthOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,1,\,-1\right),\,\mathrm{timeOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,1,\,-1\right)\right) = \left(2,\,0,\,0\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{some\_targets\_have\_no\_solution\_at\_all}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_looser\_condition\_returns\_many}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{R.flatMap}\mathopen{}\left(A \mapsto \{\, \left(A,\,D\right) \mid D \in \mathrm{R.filter}\mathopen{}\left(D \mapsto \mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(A,\,0,\,D\right) = 0\right) \,\}\right)\right| = 19 \land \left|\mathrm{R.flatMap}\mathopen{}\left(A \mapsto \{\, \left(A,\,D\right) \mid D \in \mathrm{R.filter}\mathopen{}\left(D \mapsto \mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(A,\,0,\,D\right) = 0\right) \,\}\right)\right| > 1 \land \left|\mathrm{R.filter}\mathopen{}\left(A \mapsto \mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(A,\,0,\,0\right) = 0\right)\right| = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_three\_constants\_are\_dimensionally\_independent}]
\[ \mathrm{solutions}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,0,\,0\right) = [\left(0,\,0,\,0\right)] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_coefficient\_is\_undetermined\_and\_the\_exponents\_are\_not}]
\[ \mathrm{solutions}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,2,\,0\right) = [\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right)] \land \mathrm{lengthOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right) = 2 \land \mathrm{massOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right) = 0 \land \mathrm{timeOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,-3,\,1\right) = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{discount.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_discount\_never\_raises\_the\_figure}]
\[ \forall \mathrm{raw} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \forall \mathrm{fl} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{raw},\,\mathrm{fl}\right) \le \mathrm{raw} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 900 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_figure\_at\_or\_below\_its\_floor\_is\_worth\_nothing}]
\[ \forall \mathrm{raw} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \forall \mathrm{fl} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{raw} \le \mathrm{fl} = \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{raw},\,\mathrm{fl}\right) = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 900 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_figure\_above\_its\_floor\_is\_worth\_something}]
\[ \forall \mathrm{raw} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \forall \mathrm{fl} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{raw} > \mathrm{fl} = \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{raw},\,\mathrm{fl}\right) > 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 900 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_discount\_is\_exactly\_the\_excess\_over\_the\_control}]
\[ \forall \mathrm{raw} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \forall \mathrm{fl} \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{raw},\,\mathrm{fl}\right) + \mathrm{min}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{raw},\,\mathrm{fl}\right) = \mathrm{raw} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 900 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_matcher\_is\_blind\_below\_its\_floor\_and\_not\_above}]
\[ \forall \mathrm{fl} \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \lnot a \le \mathrm{fl} \land b \le \mathrm{fl} \lor \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,\mathrm{fl}\right) = \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,\mathrm{fl}\right) \land \lnot a > \mathrm{fl} \land b > \mathrm{fl} \land a \neq b \lor \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,\mathrm{fl}\right) \neq \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,\mathrm{fl}\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_measured\_ring\_clears\_its\_floor\_by\_eleven}]
\[ \mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(15,\,4\right) = 11 \land \frac{\mathrm{discounted}\mathopen{}\left(15,\,4\right) \cdot 100}{28} = 39 \land \frac{15 \cdot 100}{28} = 53 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{domain.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_sequence\_is\_eleven\_positions\_that\_return\_to\_one}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{sequence}\right| = 11 \land \operatorname{head}\left(\mathrm{sequence}\right) = \operatorname{last}\left(\mathrm{sequence}\right) \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{sequence}\right)\right| = 10 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, d \bmod 9 \mid d \in \mathrm{sequence} \,\}\right)\right| = 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nine\_folds\_to\_zero\_and\_reflects\_to\_one}]
\texttt{9 \% 9 == 0 \&\& refl 9 == 1 \&\& sequence.getD 8 0 == 9 \&\& sequence.getD 9 0 == 0 \&\& sequence.getD 10 0 == 1}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_sequence\_exceeds\_the\_residues\_by\_the\_fold\_and\_the\_return}]
\texttt{sequence.length - residues.length == 2 \&\& residues.length == 9 \&\& sequence.drop 9 == [0, 1] \&\& (sequence.drop 9).map refl == [10, 9]}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_diagonal\_escapes\_nine\_over\_nine\_and\_eleven\_over\_eleven}]
\[ \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(9,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right) \land \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(11,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(11\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_diagonal\_fails\_on\_a\_list\_one\_longer\_than\_its\_domain}]
\[ \lnot \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(9,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mathbin{+\!\!+} [\mathrm{diagOver}\mathopen{}\left(9,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right)]\right) \land \lnot \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(11,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(11\right) \mathbin{+\!\!+} [\mathrm{diagOver}\mathopen{}\left(11,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(11\right)\right)]\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_reach\_of\_the\_diagonal\_is\_the\_length\_of\_its\_domain}]
\texttt{((List.range 12).drop 1).all (fun n => escapes n (identityRows n) \&\& !escapes n (identityRows n ++ [diagOver n (identityRows n)]))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_eleven\_positions\_are\_a\_walk\_of\_nine\_residues\_that\_returns}]
\texttt{(sequence.map (fun d => d \% 9)).eraseDups.length == 9 \&\& (sequence.map (fun d => d \% 9)).getD 8 99 == (sequence.map (fun d => d \% 9)).getD 9 88 \&\& (sequence.map (fun d => d \% 9)).getD 0 77 == (sequence.map (fun d => d \% 9)).getD 10 66}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_diagonal\_does\_not\_run\_out\_at\_nine}]
\[ \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(9,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right) \land \lnot \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(9,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mathbin{+\!\!+} [\mathrm{diagOver}\mathopen{}\left(9,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right)]\right) \land \mathrm{escapes}\mathopen{}\left(11,\,\mathrm{identityRows}\mathopen{}\left(11\right)\right) \land 11 > 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{elementary.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{euclids\_form\_is\_perfect\_at\_every\_mersenne\_prime\_to\_seven}]
\[ \forall p \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7],\; \lnot \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(2^{p} - 1\right) \lor \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(2^{p - 1} \cdot 2^{p} - 1\right) = 2^{p - 1} \cdot 2^{p} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mersenne\_hypothesis\_is\_real\_at\_eleven}]
