\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_amicable\_pair\_is\_mutual\_and\_neither\_is\_perfect}]
\[ \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(220\right) = 284 \land \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(284\right) = 220 \land \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(220\right) \neq 220 \land \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(284\right) \neq 284 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_primitive\_roots\_mod\_nine\_are\_exactly\_two\_and\_five}]
\[ \{\, g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mid \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,9\right) = 6 \,\} = [2,\,5] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 2 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_eisenstein\_units\_are\_exactly\_six}]
\texttt{(((List.range 7).flatMap (fun (i : Nat) => (List.range 7).map (fun (j : Nat) => (((i : Int) - 3, (j : Int) - 3) : Int × Int)))).filter (fun p => eisensteinNorm p.1 p.2 == 1)).length = 6}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 49 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_subtraction\_game\_loses\_exactly\_at\_the\_multiples\_of\_four}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,40\},\; \begin{cases}\forall m \in [1,\,2,\,3],\; \lnot m \le n \lor n - m \bmod 4 \neq 0 & \text{if } n \bmod 4 = 0\\ \exists m \in [1,\,2,\,3] : m \le n \land n - m \bmod 4 = 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 369 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_of\_z9\_form\_three\_pairs\_summing\_to\_nine}]
\[ \left|\{\, u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mid u < 9 - u \,\}\right| = 3 \land \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; 9 - u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land u + 9 - u = 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_multiplication\_table\_of\_the\_z9\_units\_is\_a\_latin\_square}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid b \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right| \land \forall x \in \{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid b \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land \forall b \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid a \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right| \land \forall x \in \{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid a \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_count\_of\_primitive\_roots\_is\_phi\_of\_phi}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,60\},\; \lnot \mathrm{hasPrimitiveRoot}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \lor \left|\{\, g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \mid \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,n\right) = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right| \,\}\right| = \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 59 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_order\_spectrum\_of\_the\_z9\_units\_realises\_gauss\_divisor\_sum}]
\[ \forall d \in \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,6\} \mid 6 \bmod d = 0 \,\},\; \left|\{\, g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mid \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,9\right) = d \,\}\right| = \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \land \sum \mathrm{totient}[\{\, d \in \{1,\dots,6\} \mid 6 \bmod d = 0 \,\}] = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 36 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_product\_of\_the\_units\_is\_minus\_one\_exactly\_where\_gauss\_says}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,60\},\; \operatorname{fold}_{a,\,u \mapsto a \cdot u \bmod n}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(n\right),\, 1\right) = \begin{cases}n - 1 & \text{if } \mathrm{gaussCyclic}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\\ 1 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 59 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_is\_a\_primitive\_root\_mod\_seven\_and\_seven\_is\_six\_plus\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,7\right) = 6 \land \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(7\right)\right| = 6 \land \left|\{0,\dots,6\}\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(7\right)\right| + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_z9\_unit\_group\_splits\_as\_reflection\_times\_trinity}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,3,\,9\right) \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,4,\,9\right) \bmod 9 = u \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,3,\,9\right) \mid u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = 2 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,4,\,9\right) \mid u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_odd\_prime\_is\_a\_sum\_of\_two\_squares\_exactly\_when\_it\_is\_one\_mod\_four}]
\[ \mathrm{isSumOfTwoSquares}\mathopen{}\left(2\right) \land \forall p \in \{\, x \in \{3,\dots,199\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; \mathrm{isSumOfTwoSquares}\mathopen{}\left(p\right) = p \bmod 4 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 197 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_area\_of\_an\_integer\_right\_triangle\_is\_a\_multiple\_of\_six}]
\[ \forall a \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \lnot \exists c \in \{1,\dots,60\} : a \cdot a + b \cdot b = c \cdot c \lor \frac{a \cdot b}{2} \bmod 6 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 96,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_inverse\_map\_permutes\_the\_units\_and\_is\_an\_involution}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{invUnit}[\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)]\right)\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right| \land \forall x \in \mathrm{invUnit}[\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)],\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \mathrm{invUnit}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{invUnit}\mathopen{}\left(u\right)\right) = u \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_hockey\_stick\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall r \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,18\},\; \sum \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(i,\,r\right) \mid i \in \{r,\dots,r+m + 1-1\} \,\} = \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(r + m + 1,\,r + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 209 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_is\_the\_only\_prime\_one\_less\_than\_a\_square\_in\_this\_range}]
\[ \{\, n \in \{2,\dots,41\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(n \cdot n - 1\right) \,\} = [2] \land \forall n \in \{3,\dots,41\},\; n - 1 \cdot n + 1 = n \cdot n - 1 \land n - 1 > 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,560 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{eight\_times\_a\_triangular\_number\_plus\_one\_is\_an\_odd\_square}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,59\},\; 8 \cdot \frac{n \cdot n + 1}{2} + 1 = 2 \cdot n + 1 \cdot 2 \cdot n + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_powers\_of\_two\_mod\_nine\_and\_of\_three\_mod\_seven\_are\_isomorphic}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,k,\,9\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,k,\,7\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,k,\,9\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,k,\,7\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(7\right) \land \forall i \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall j \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,i,\,9\right) \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,j,\,9\right) \bmod 9 = \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,i + j \bmod 6,\,9\right) \land \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,i,\,7\right) \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,j,\,7\right) \bmod 7 = \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,i + j \bmod 6,\,7\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 46,656 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_lucas\_numbers\_follow\_their\_recurrence\_and\_reach\_their\_named\_values}]
\[ \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(0\right) = 2 \land \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(1\right) = 1 \land \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 11 \land \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(7\right) = 29 \land \forall n \in \{2,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 28 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_lucas\_numbers\_are\_the\_sum\_of\_the\_neighbouring\_fibonaccis}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,28\},\; \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 28 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pell\_numbers\_follow\_their\_recurrence\_and\_reach\_their\_named\_values}]
\[ \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 29 \land \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = 70 \land \forall n \in \{2,\dots,25\},\; \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = 2 \cdot \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 24 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{consecutive\_farey\_neighbours\_have\_unit\_determinant\_and\_bracket\_their\_mediant}]
\texttt{(List.range ((farey 4).length - 1)).all (fun i => let x := (farey 4).getD i (0, 1); let y := (farey 4).getD (i + 1) (0, 1) x.2 * y.1 == x.1 * y.2 + 1) ∧ (List.range ((farey 6).length - 1)).all (fun i => let x := (farey 6).getD i (0, 1); let y := (farey 6).getD (i + 1) (0, 1) let m := (x.1 + y.1, x.2 + y.2) x.1 * m.2 < m.1 * x.2 \&\& m.1 * y.2 < y.1 * m.2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_are\_three\_regular\_under\_doubling\_halving\_and\_reflection}]
\[ \mathrm{degreeSet}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right) = [3] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_whole\_ring\_is\_not\_regular\_and\_the\_triad\_is\_why}]
\[ \mathrm{degreeSet}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,8\}\right) \neq [3] \land \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,8\},\,0\right)\right| = 0 \land \forall d \in \left([3,\,6] : \mathrm{List}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{Nat}\right)\right),\; \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,8\},\,d\right)\right| = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,458 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_interior\_and\_exterior\_angles\_are\_n\_straight\_angles}]
\[ \forall n \in \{3,\dots,32\},\; n - 2 \cdot 180 + 360 = n \cdot 180 \land \frac{180 \cdot 3 - 2}{3} = 60 \land \frac{180 \cdot 5 - 2}{5} = 108 \land \frac{180 \cdot 8 - 2}{8} = 135 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_knight\_has\_exactly\_eight\_leaps\_and\_every\_one\_flips\_the\_colour}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{knightSteps}\right| = 8 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{knightSteps}\right)\right| = 8 \land \forall p \in \mathrm{knightSteps},\; p_{1} + p_{2} \bmod 2 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_golden\_beatty\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{upperWythoff}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) - \mathrm{lowerWythoff}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_wythoff\_sequences\_partition\_the\_integers}]
\texttt{(List.range' 1 50).all (fun m => ((List.range' 1 40).map lowerWythoff).count m + ((List.range' 1 40).map upperWythoff).count m == 1)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 80,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_partition\_and\_bell\_numbers\_count\_what\_they\_claim\_to\_count}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{partitionsOf}\mathopen{}\left(5\right)\right| = 7 \land \left|\mathrm{partitionsOf}\mathopen{}\left(7\right)\right| = 15 \land \left|\mathrm{partitionsOf}\mathopen{}\left(10\right)\right| = 42 \land \forall n \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \left|\mathrm{rgs}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right| = \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \land \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(3\right) = 5 \land \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) = 15 \land \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 52 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unit\_graph\_is\_complete\_bipartite\_between\_the\_two\_tetrahedra}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(1\right)\right| = 3 \land \left|\mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(2\right)\right| = 3 \land \forall d \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(1\right),\; \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right)\right| = 3 \land \forall t \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; t \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(2\right) \land \forall d \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(2\right),\; \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right)\right| = 3 \land \forall t \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; t \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(1\right) \land \forall d \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \forall x \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; \forall y \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; x = y \lor \lnot y \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,x\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{elementary\_settles\_its\_range}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_interior\_and\_exterior\_angles\_are\_n\_straight\_angles\_for\_every\_n}]

\paragraph{\texttt{families.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{flt\_all\_primes\_under\_thirty}]
\[ \forall p \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30},\; \forall a \in \{1,\dots,1+p - 1-1\},\; a^{p - 1} \bmod p = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{wilson\_all\_primes\_under\_thirty}]
\[ \forall p \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30},\; \mathrm{fact}\mathopen{}\left(p - 1\right) \bmod p = p - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{wilson\_fails\_at\_composites}]
\[ \forall n \in \{\, n \in \{4,\dots,23\} \mid \lnot n \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30} \,\},\; \mathrm{fact}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \bmod n \neq n - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pascal\_rows\_sum\_to\_powers\_of\_two}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \sum \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = 2^{n} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pascal\_alternating\_sums\_vanish}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,11\},\; \sum \{\, \begin{cases}\mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) & \text{if } k \bmod 2 = 0\\ 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = \sum \{\, \begin{cases}\mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) & \text{if } k \bmod 2 = 1\\ 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{totient\_at\_prime\_powers}]
\[ \forall p \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7],\; \forall k \in \{1,\dots,3\},\; \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(p^{k}\right) = p^{k} - p^{k - 1} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{geometric\_series\_all\_bases}]
\[ \forall b \in \{2,\dots,9\},\; \sum \{\, b^{i} \mid i \in \{0,\dots,4\} \,\} \cdot b - 1 = b^{5} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_is\_parity\_up\_to\_eight\_bits}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,2^{k}-1\},\; \mathrm{popcount}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 2 = \operatorname{fold}_{\mathrm{acc},\,i \mapsto \mathrm{acc} + n \gg i \bmod 2 \bmod 2}\left(\{0,\dots,k-1\},\, 0\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nat\_div\_is\_a\_total\_function\_returning\_zero\_at\_a\_zero\_divisor}]
\[ \frac{7}{0} = 0 \land \frac{0}{0} = 0 \land 7 \bmod 0 = 7 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{division\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,12\},\; \frac{12}{d} \cdot d + 12 \bmod d = 12 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{at\_a\_zero\_divisor\_the\_identity\_is\_carried\_by\_the\_remainder}]
\[ \frac{12}{0} = 0 \land 12 \bmod 0 = 12 \land \frac{12}{0} \cdot 0 + 12 \bmod 0 = 12 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{primality\_is\_decided\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,18\},\; \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) = m \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 17 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_are\_cyclic\_at\_exactly\_the\_gauss\_moduli}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,18\},\; \mathrm{hasPrimitiveRoot}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) = \mathrm{gaussCyclic}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 17 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{demorgan\_holds\_at\_every\_arity\_to\_eight}]
\[ \forall k \in \{2,\dots,8\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,2^{k}-1\},\; \lnot \forall i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\},\; n \gg i \bmod 2 = 1 = \exists i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\} : n \gg i \bmod 2 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{multiplication\_permutes\_z9\_at\_exactly\_the\_units}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{permutesZ9}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) = \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addition\_generates\_z9\_at\_exactly\_the\_units}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{addGeneratesZ9}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) = \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_inverse\_mod\_nine\_exists\_at\_exactly\_the\_units}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \exists\,\mathrm{invOf}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(d,\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_inverse\_of\_a\_unit\_mod\_nine\_is\_its\_fifth\_power}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; u \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,5,\,9\right) \bmod 9 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{power\_sums\_match\_their\_closed\_forms}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,5\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{powSum}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) = \mathrm{faulhaber}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 205 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digital\_root\_is\_invariant\_under\_digit\_reversal}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \mathrm{digitalRoot}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right) = \mathrm{digitalRoot}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{reverseDigits}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{geometric\_series\_across\_bases\_and\_exponents}]
\[ \forall b \in \{2,\dots,12\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \sum \{\, b^{i} \mid i \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} \cdot b - 1 = b^{n + 1} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 77 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{totient\_at\_prime\_powers\_through\_thirteen}]
\[ \forall p \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7,\,11,\,13],\; \forall k \in \{1,\dots,3\},\; \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(p^{k}\right) = p^{k} - p^{k - 1} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 18 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_powers\_of\_a\_unit\_sum\_to\_zero\_when\_g\_minus\_one\_is\_invertible}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,20\},\; \forall g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(m\right),\; \lnot \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(g - 1,\,m\right) = 1 \lor \mathrm{rootSum}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,m\right) \bmod m = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 19 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{and\_the\_converse\_fails\_at\_six}]
\[ \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(5,\,6\right) = 2 \land \mathrm{rootSum}\mathopen{}\left(5,\,6\right) \bmod 6 = 0 \land \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,6\right) = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_n\_odd\_numbers\_sum\_to\_n\_squared}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,200\},\; \sum \{\, 2 \cdot i + 1 \mid i \in \{0,\dots,n-1\} \,\} = n \cdot n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 201 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_powers\_of\_two\_sum\_to\_one\_less\_than\_the\_next}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,40\},\; \sum \{\, 2^{i} \mid i \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = 2^{n + 1} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 41 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_sums\_of\_triangular\_numbers\_are\_the\_tetrahedral\_numbers}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,100\},\; \sum \{\, \frac{i \cdot i + 1}{2} \mid i \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \,\} = \frac{n \cdot n + 1 \cdot n + 2}{6} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 101 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_squares\_of\_a\_pascal\_row\_sum\_to\_the\_central\_binomial}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,12\},\; \sum \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \cdot \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot n,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{polygonal\_closed\_forms\_match\_their\_recurrences}]
\[ \forall s \in \{3,\dots,10\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,50\},\; \frac{s - 2 \cdot n \cdot n + 4 \cdot n - s \cdot n}{2} = \mathrm{polyRec}\mathopen{}\left(s,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 408 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_order\_of\_every\_unit\_mod\_nine\_divides\_six}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,9\right) > 0 \land 6 \bmod \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,9\right) = 0 \land \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,\mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,9\right),\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_square\_iteration\_reaches\_one\_or\_four}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{iterF}\mathopen{}\left(60,\,n\right) = 1 \lor \mathrm{iterF}\mathopen{}\left(60,\,n\right) = 4 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_number\_is\_a\_sum\_of\_two\_squares\_exactly\_when\_fermats\_condition\_holds}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{isSumOfTwoSquares}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{everyThreeModFourPrimeIsEven}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_three\_power\_sums\_hold\_to\_two\_hundred}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,3\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{powSum}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) = \mathrm{faulhaber}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 603 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{there\_are\_exactly\_five\_platonic\_solids\_and\_each\_satisfies\_eulers\_formula}]
\texttt{schlafli.length = 5 ∧ schlafli.all (fun x => let d := 2 * x.1 + 2 * x.2 - x.1 * x.2 4 * x.1 \% d == 0 \&\& 2 * x.1 * x.2 \% d == 0 \&\& 4 * x.2 \% d == 0 \&\& 4 * x.1 / d + 4 * x.2 / d == 2 * x.1 * x.2 / d + 2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassinis\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \begin{cases}\mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) + 1 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_golden\_convergents\_are\_fibonacci\_ratios\_with\_unit\_determinant}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,24\},\; \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 2\right) \land \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) \land \begin{cases}\mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} + 1 = \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} + \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} = \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} + \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} + 1 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 25 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_catalan\_recurrence\_matches\_the\_binomial\_formula}]
\texttt{(List.range 13).all (fun n => (catalanList 12).getD n 0 == choose (2 * n) n / (n + 1))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pisano\_period\_of\_nine\_is\_twenty\_four\_and\_every\_modulus\_to\_twelve\_has\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) = 24 \land \forall m \in \{2,\dots,12\},\; \mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) > 0 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(m\right)\right) \bmod m = 0 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) + 1\right) \bmod m = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_sum\_rules\_for\_three\_and\_nine\_hold\_below\_ten\_thousand}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right) \bmod 3 = 0 = a \cdot 100 + b \bmod 3 = 0 \land \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right) \bmod 9 = 0 = a \cdot 100 + b \bmod 9 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_involutions\_are\_counted\_by\_the\_telephone\_numbers}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \left|\{\, x \in \mathrm{permsN}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,n-1\}\right) \mid \mathrm{isInvolution}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| = \mathrm{telephone}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{invOf\_is\_an\_inverse\_and\_an\_involution\_on\_the\_units}]
\texttt{(List.range 9).all (fun d => match invOf d with | some e => d * e \% 9 == 1 \&\& invOf e == some d | none => gcd' d 9 != 1)}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_ring\_takes\_one\_value\_and\_the\_control\_takes\_three}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{no\_label\_of\_the\_ring\_narrows\_the\_subject}]
\[ \forall d \in \mathrm{labels},\; \left|\{\, h \in \mathrm{hubs} \mid d \in \mathrm{ring}\mathopen{}\left(h\right) \,\}\right| = \left|\mathrm{hubs}\right| \lor \left|\{\, h \in \mathrm{hubs} \mid d \in \mathrm{ring}\mathopen{}\left(h\right) \,\}\right| = 0 \]
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_label\_of\_the\_varying\_reading\_names\_one\_subject}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_printed\_balance\_holds\_only\_when\_the\_soil\_is\_zero}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_intended\_update\_is\_injective\_in\_the\_soil}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13,824 cases. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{sequences.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassini\_at\_even\_indices}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; n \bmod 2 = 1 \lor \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassini\_at\_odd\_indices}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; n \bmod 2 = 0 \lor \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) + 1 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassini\_deviation\_is\_exactly\_one}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 \lor \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) + 1 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{lucas\_mod\_two\_is\_the\_and\_rule}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,13\},\; \forall k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\},\; \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \bmod 2 = 1 = \mathrm{andN}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) = k \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 14 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pascal\_has\_both\_parities}]
\[ 1 \in \bigcup_{n \in \{0,\dots,13\}} \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \bmod 2 \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} \land 0 \in \bigcup_{n \in \{0,\dots,13\}} \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \bmod 2 \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 196 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{sums\_of\_two\_squares\_are\_closed}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \forall d \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; a \cdot a + b \cdot b \cdot c \cdot c + d \cdot d = a \cdot c - b \cdot d \cdot a \cdot c - b \cdot d + a \cdot d + b \cdot c \cdot a \cdot d + b \cdot c \lor a \cdot c < b \cdot d \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,096 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_five\_eight\_are\_consecutive}]
\[ \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) = 3 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 5 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = 8 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) + \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pisano\_twentyfour\_is\_four\_sixes}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) \bmod 9 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(k + 24\right) \bmod 9 \land 24 = 4 \cdot 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{thue\_morse\_doubling\_recurrence}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,199\},\; \mathrm{tm}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot n\right) = \mathrm{tm}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \land \mathrm{tm}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot n + 1\right) = 1 - \mathrm{tm}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{doubling\_preserves\_the\_parity\_of\_the\_bits}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,199\},\; \mathrm{popcount}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot n\right) = \mathrm{popcount}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_odd\_step\_sets\_exactly\_one\_further\_bit}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,199\},\; \mathrm{popcount}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot n + 1\right) = \mathrm{popcount}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_sequence\_takes\_both\_values}]
\[ \left|\{\, n \in \{0,\dots,199\} \mid \mathrm{tm}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = 0 \,\}\right| = 100 \land \left|\{\, n \in \{0,\dots,199\} \mid \mathrm{tm}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = 1 \,\}\right| = 100 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassinis\_identity\_for\_every\_m}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_bit\_sum\_over\_one\_more\_position\_adds\_that\_bit}]
\texttt{∀ F m : Nat, (List.range (F + 1)).foldl (fun a i => a + m / 2 ^ i \% 2) 0 = (List.range F).foldl (fun a i => a + m / 2 ^ i \% 2) 0 + m / 2 ^ F \% 2}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{thue\_morse\_doubling\_recurrence\_for\_every\_n}]

\paragraph{\texttt{split.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_singles\_are\_exactly\_the\_non\_units}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,9\} \mid \lnot \mathrm{isUnit9}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \,\} = [0,\,3,\,6,\,9] \land \mathrm{singles} = [0,\,3,\,6,\,9] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 160 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pairs\_are\_exactly\_the\_units\_in\_order}]
\[ \{\, x \in \{0,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{isUnit9}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\} = \mathrm{unitsOf9} \land \mathrm{unitsOf9} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{each\_pair\_is\_two\_consecutive\_units}]
\[ 12 = 1 \cdot 10 + 2 \land 45 = 4 \cdot 10 + 5 \land 78 = 7 \cdot 10 + 8 \land 2 = 1 + 1 \land 5 = 4 + 1 \land 8 = 7 + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_token\_is\_a\_multiple\_of\_three}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_tokens\_are\_three\_times\_these}]
\[ \{\, \frac{t}{3} \mid t \in \mathrm{tokens} \,\} = [0,\,4,\,1,\,15,\,2,\,26,\,3] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addition\_and\_multiplication\_stay\_inside}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{tokens},\; \forall b \in \mathrm{tokens},\; a + b \bmod 3 = 0 \land a \cdot b \bmod 3 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{subtraction\_stays\_inside}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{tokens},\; \forall b \in \mathrm{tokens},\; a < b \lor a - b \bmod 3 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{division\_is\_the\_operation\_that\_leaves}]
\[ \frac{12}{3} = 4 \land 4 \bmod 3 \neq 0 \land \frac{45}{9} = 5 \land 5 \bmod 3 \neq 0 \land \frac{6}{3} = 2 \land 2 \bmod 3 \neq 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_roots\_are\_the\_singles}]
\[ \{\, t \bmod 9 \mid t \in \mathrm{tokens} \,\} = [0,\,3,\,3,\,0,\,6,\,6,\,0] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_root\_is\_a\_single}]
\[ \forall t \in \mathrm{tokens},\; \begin{cases}\begin{cases}0 & \text{if } t = 0\\ 9 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} & \text{if } t \bmod 9 = 0\\ t \bmod 9 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \in \mathrm{singles} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_coin\_step\_and\_the\_coins\_are\_inverse}]
\[ \forall c \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; \frac{3 \cdot c}{3} = c \land 3 \cdot c \bmod 3 = 0 \land \mathrm{coinStep} = 3 \cdot \mathrm{coins} \land \frac{\mathrm{coinStep}}{3} = \mathrm{coins} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{paying\_and\_refunding\_coins\_on\_the\_last\_pair\_round\_trips}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,26\},\; 3 \cdot 26 - k + 3 \cdot k = 78 \land 3 \cdot 26 - \mathrm{coins} = 72 \land 72 = 8 \cdot 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 27 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_exhaustible\_tokens\_are\_those\_six\_divides}]
\[ \{\, t \in \mathrm{tokens} \mid t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} = 0 \,\} = [0,\,12,\,6,\,78] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_rest\_halt\_on\_the\_generator}]
\[ \{\, t \in \mathrm{tokens} \mid t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} \neq 0 \,\} = [3,\,45,\,9] \land \forall t \in \{\, t \in \mathrm{tokens} \mid t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} \neq 0 \,\},\; t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} = 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 3 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_token\_is\_void\_bound\_or\_halts\_on\_three}]
\[ \forall t \in \mathrm{tokens},\; t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} = 0 \lor t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} = 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{inside\_this\_ideal\_the\_bare\_coin\_sorts\_as\_the\_scaled\_one}]
\[ \{\, t \in \mathrm{tokens} \mid t \bmod 2 = 0 \,\} = \{\, t \in \mathrm{tokens} \mid t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} = 0 \,\} \land \forall t \in \mathrm{tokens},\; t \bmod 2 = 0 = t \bmod \mathrm{coinStep} = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_seal\_divides\_into\_payments\_and\_multiplies\_back}]
\[ \forall c \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,16,\,32,\,64,\,128],\; \frac{\mathrm{sealBits}}{c} \cdot c = \mathrm{sealBits} \land \frac{\mathrm{sealBits}}{\frac{\mathrm{sealBits}}{c}} = c \land \frac{\mathrm{sealBits}}{\mathrm{coins}} = 2^{6} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{sixty\_four\_is\_where\_the\_doubling\_returns}]
\[ 2^{6} \bmod 9 = 1 \land \{\, 2^{k} \bmod 9 \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \land \forall k \in \{1,\dots,5\},\; 2^{k} \bmod 9 \neq 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 180 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_orbit\_turns\_once\_per\_budget}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,59\},\; 2^{n + 6} \bmod 9 = 2^{n} \bmod 9 \land 2^{6} = \frac{\mathrm{sealBits}}{\mathrm{coins}} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_coins\_return\_from\_the\_coin\_step\_for\_every\_multiple\_of\_three}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{paying\_and\_refunding\_round\_trips\_for\_every\_count}]

\end{theorem}

\paragraph{\texttt{turns.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{doubling\_returns\_in\_six\_two\_or\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl}\right)[\mathrm{ring}] = [1,\,6,\,6,\,2,\,6,\,6,\,2,\,6,\,6] \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl}\right)[\mathrm{ring}]\right)\right| = 3 \land 6 + 2 + 1 = 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_long\_loop\_is\_the\_span\_and\_the\_short\_one\_is\_the\_triad\_pair}]
\[ \{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 6 \,\} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 2 \,\} = [3,\,6] \land \{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 1 \,\} = [0] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{both\_loops\_complete\_three\_hundred\_and\_sixty\_degrees}]
\[ \left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) > 1 \,\}\right| = 8 \land \frac{360}{6} = 60 \land \frac{360}{2} = 180 \land 6 \cdot 60 = 360 \land 2 \cdot 180 = 360 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_steps\_of\_the\_long\_loop\_are\_one\_step\_of\_the\_short}]
\[ 3 \cdot 60 = 180 \land 2 \cdot 90 = 180 \land 180 \cdot 2 = 360 \land \frac{6}{2} = 3 \land \mathrm{iter}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,1,\,3\right) = 8 \land \mathrm{iter}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,3,\,1\right) = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_orbits\_are\_disjoint\_and\_cover\_the\_ring}]
\[ \left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 6 \,\}\right| + \left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 2 \,\}\right| + \left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 1 \,\}\right| = 9 \land \forall d \in \mathrm{ring},\; \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 6 \lor \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 2 \lor \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{dbl},\,d\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_reflection\_leaves\_four\_loops\_on\_the\_same\_ring}]
\[ \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{rfl9}\right)[\mathrm{ring}] = [2,\,2,\,2,\,2,\,2,\,1,\,2,\,2,\,2] \land \left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{rfl9},\,d\right) = 2 \,\}\right| = 8 \land \frac{\left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{ring} \mid \mathrm{cycLen}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{rfl9},\,d\right) = 2 \,\}\right|}{2} = 4 \land 4 \neq 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{no\_orbit\_fails\_to\_return\_under\_either\_map}]

\paragraph{\texttt{generated.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{powsum\_zero\_odd\_exponents}]
\[ \forall k \in [1,\,3,\,5,\,7,\,9,\,11,\,13,\,15,\,17],\; \sum \{\, u^{k} \bmod 9 \mid u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} \bmod 9 = 0 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{powsum\_nonzero\_at\_even\_exponents}]
\[ \lnot \forall k \in [2,\,4,\,6],\; \sum \{\, u^{k} \bmod 9 \mid u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} \bmod 9 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 18 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{mulperm\_iff\_unit\_all}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, k \cdot u \bmod 9 \mid u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 = k \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 324 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{mulperm\_fails\_at\_the\_triad}]
\[ \lnot \exists k \in [0,\,3,\,6] : \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, k \cdot u \bmod 9 \mid u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 18 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addgen\_iff\_coprime\_all}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, k \cdot i \bmod 9 \mid i \in \{0,\dots,8\} \,\}\right)\right| = 9 = k \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 486 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{hasinv\_iff\_unit\_all}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \exists e \in \{0,\dots,8\} : d \cdot e \bmod 9 = 1 = d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 486 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{demorgan\_all\_widths}]
\[ \forall k \in \{2,\dots,8\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,2^{k}-1\},\; \lnot \forall i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\},\; n \gg i \bmod 2 = 1 = \exists i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\} : n \gg i \bmod 2 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{power\_sum\_closed\_forms}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \sum \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \cdot 2 = n \cdot n + 1 \land \sum \{\, i \cdot i \mid i \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \,\} \cdot 6 = n \cdot n + 1 \cdot 2 \cdot n + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{decimal\_period\_is\_the\_order\_of\_ten}]
\texttt{[3,7,11,13,17,19,23,29].all (fun p => ((List.range' 1 (p - 1)).find? (fun k => ((List.range k).foldl (fun r \_ => r * 10 \% p) 1) == 1)).getD 0 == ((List.range' 1 (p - 1)).find? (fun k => (10 ^ k) \% p == 1)).getD 0)}

\paragraph{\texttt{nim.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{bouton\_two\_heaps\_lost\_iff\_xor\_zero}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \mathrm{isLost}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{bouton\_lost\_iff\_heaps\_equal}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \mathrm{isLost}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = a = b \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{lost\_positions\_are\_exactly\_the\_diagonal}]
\[ \left|\{\, p \in \bigcup_{a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\}} \{\, \left(a,\,b\right) \mid b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\} \,\} \mid \mathrm{isLost}\mathopen{}\left(p_{1},\,p_{2}\right) \,\}\right| = N \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{grundy\_of\_a\_single\_heap\_is\_its\_size}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \mathrm{grundy1}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1,\,n\right) = n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{grundy\_of\_two\_heaps\_is\_the\_xor}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{grundy1}\mathopen{}\left(a + 1,\,a\right),\,\mathrm{grundy1}\mathopen{}\left(b + 1,\,b\right)\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_is\_its\_own\_inverse}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right),\,b\right) = a \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_is\_commutative}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_zero\_is\_identity}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,31\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,0\right) = a \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 32 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nim\_sum\_is\_a\_gf2\_vector\_addition}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right),\,c\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,c\right)\right) \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right),\,b\right) = a \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,0\right) = a \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4,294,967,296 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{bouton\_holds\_at\_every\_heap\_size\_to\_six}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \mathrm{isLost}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = 0 \]

\paragraph{\texttt{preimage.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_single\_leaf\_folds\_to\_itself}]
\[ \forall x \in \{0,\dots,39\},\; \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([x]\right) = x \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_two\_leaf\_root\_is\_also\_a\_one\_leaf\_root}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([\mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([a,\,b]\right)]\right) = \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([a,\,b]\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 400 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_forged\_leaf\_set\_is\_not\_the\_original}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; [\mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([a,\,b]\right)] \neq [a,\,b] \land \left|[\mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([a,\,b]\right)]\right| \neq \left|[a,\,b]\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 400 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_empty\_sentinel\_is\_also\_a\_one\_leaf\_root}]
\[ \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([]\right) = \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([7]\right) \land \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([]\right) = 7 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{distinct\_pairs\_still\_give\_distinct\_roots}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; a = c \lor \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([a,\,b]\right) \neq \mathrm{foldOf}\mathopen{}\left([c,\,b]\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,728 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{tagging\_separates\_a\_leaf\_from\_a\_node}]
\[ \forall x \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \forall y \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \forall z \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{leafTag}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \neq \mathrm{nodeTag}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,z\right) \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_tagged\_fold\_refuses\_the\_forged\_leaf}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,19\},\; \mathrm{leafTag}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{nodeTag}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right)\right) \neq \mathrm{nodeTag}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 400 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unescaped\_separator\_makes\_the\_pairing\_non\_injective}]
\[ \mathrm{mergeStr}\mathopen{}\left([1,\,\mathrm{sep},\,2],\,[3]\right) = \mathrm{mergeStr}\mathopen{}\left([1],\,[2,\,\mathrm{sep},\,3]\right) \land [1,\,\mathrm{sep},\,2] \neq \left([1] : \mathrm{List}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{Nat}\right)\right) \land \left([3] : \mathrm{List}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{Nat}\right)\right) \neq [2,\,\mathrm{sep},\,3] \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; b = c \lor \mathrm{mergeStr}\mathopen{}\left([a],\,[b]\right) \neq \mathrm{mergeStr}\mathopen{}\left([a],\,[c]\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,728 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_reflection\_involution}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \land \left|\{\, d \in \{0,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \,\}\right| = 1 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_pairs\_sum\_to\_ten}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_reflection\_is\_not\_an\_involution\_above\_ten}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_z9\_reflection\_permutes\_the\_units}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_millennium\_reflection\_escapes\_the\_units}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 36 cases. \hfill$\square$

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{reversal.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_preserves\_digit\_sum}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,300\},\; \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) = \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 300 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{digital\_root\_is\_invariant\_under\_reversal}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,300\},\; \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 9 = n \bmod 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 300 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_ledger\_reversal\_cases}]
\[ \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(12\right) \bmod 9 = 12 \bmod 9 \land \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(45\right) \bmod 9 = 45 \bmod 9 \land \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(123\right) \bmod 9 = 123 \bmod 9 \land \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(1234\right) \bmod 9 = 1234 \bmod 9 \land \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(4321\right) \bmod 9 = 4321 \bmod 9 \land \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(9080\right) \bmod 9 = 9080 \bmod 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_is\_not\_the\_identity}]
\[ \lnot \forall n \in \{10,\dots,99\},\; \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 90 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{palindromes\_are\_the\_fixed\_points}]
\[ \left|\{\, n \in \{10,\dots,99\} \mid \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = n \,\}\right| = 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 90 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{emirps\_exist\_below\_one\_hundred}]
\[ \left|\{\, n \in \{10,\dots,99\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \land \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) \land \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \neq n \,\}\right| > 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 90 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_does\_not\_preserve\_primality}]
\[ \lnot \forall n \in \{10,\dots,99\},\; \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 90 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_is\_involutive\_exactly\_off\_the\_trailing\_zeros}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,300\},\; \mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{reverseNum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) = n = n \bmod 10 \neq 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 300 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_settles\_its\_range}]
\[ \mathrm{settledHere} = 8 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{rfl} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{foldl\_add\_start}]
\texttt{∀ (l : List Nat) (a : Nat), l.foldl (· + ·) a = a + l.foldl (· + ·) 0}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digits\_sum\_to\_the\_number\_mod\_nine\_when\_the\_fuel\_covers\_them}]
\texttt{∀ f n : Nat, n < 10 ^ f → (digitsF f n).foldl (· + ·) 0 \% 9 = n \% 9}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_number\_is\_below\_ten\_to\_its\_own\_successor}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n < 10 ^ (n + 1)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_sum\_has\_the\_residue\_of\_the\_number\_mod\_nine\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, digitSum n \% 9 = n \% 9}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{folding\_by\_ten\_keeps\_the\_residue\_of\_the\_digit\_sum}]
\texttt{∀ (l : List Nat) (a : Nat), (l.foldl (fun a d => a * 10 + d) a) \% 9 = (a + l.foldl (· + ·) 0) \% 9}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_keeps\_the\_residue\_mod\_nine\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, reverseNum n \% 9 = n \% 9}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{digitsF\_zero}]
\texttt{∀ f : Nat, digitsF f 0 = []}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_digit\_is\_below\_ten}]
\texttt{∀ f n : Nat, ∀ d ∈ digitsF f n, d < 10}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digits\_rebuild\_the\_number\_when\_the\_fuel\_covers\_them}]
\texttt{∀ f n : Nat, n < 10 ^ f → (digitsF f n).foldr (fun d a => a * 10 + d) 0 = n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_number\_is\_rebuilt\_from\_its\_digits\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, (digits n).foldr (fun d a => a * 10 + d) 0 = n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_sum\_is\_the\_same\_read\_backwards}]
\texttt{∀ (l : List Nat), l.reverse.foldl (· + ·) 0 = l.foldl (· + ·) 0}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_top\_digit\_is\_nonzero}]
\texttt{∀ f n : Nat, 0 < n → n < 10 ^ f → ∃ L b, digitsF f n = L ++ [b] ∧ 0 < b ∧ b < 10}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_leaves\_no\_trailing\_zero}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, 0 < n → 0 < reverseNum n ∧ reverseNum n \% 10 ≠ 0}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reading\_a\_list\_back\_from\_the\_top}]
\texttt{∀ r : List Nat, r ≠ [] → (∀ d ∈ r, d < 10) → r.reverse.head? ≠ some 0 → 0 < r.reverse.foldl (fun a d => a * 10 + d) 0 ∧ r.length ≤ r.reverse.foldl (fun a d => a * 10 + d) 0 ∧ ∀ f, r.length ≤ f → digitsF f (r.reverse.foldl (fun a d => a * 10 + d) 0) = r}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digits\_of\_the\_reversal\_are\_the\_reversed\_digits}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n \% 10 ≠ 0 → digits (reverseNum n) = (digitsF (n + 1) n).reverse ∧ digits n = digitsF (n + 1) n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_is\_involutive\_off\_the\_trailing\_zeros\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n \% 10 ≠ 0 → reverseNum (reverseNum n) = n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_is\_involutive\_exactly\_off\_the\_trailing\_zeros\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, 0 < n → (reverseNum (reverseNum n) = n ↔ n \% 10 ≠ 0)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{more\_fuel\_changes\_no\_digit}]
\texttt{∀ f n k : Nat, n < 10 ^ f → digitsF (f + k) n = digitsF f n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_trailing\_zero\_is\_a\_leading\_zero\_of\_the\_digits}]
\texttt{∀ m : Nat, 0 < m → digits (10 * m) = 0 :: digits m}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_preserves\_the\_digit\_sum\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, digitSum (reverseNum n) = digitSum n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fixed\_points\_of\_reversal\_are\_the\_palindromes\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n \% 10 ≠ 0 → (reverseNum n = n ↔ (digits n).reverse = digits n)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\subsection{the record}

\paragraph{\texttt{priorart.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{novelty\_is\_claimed\_only\_where\_a\_search\_was\_performed}]
\[ \forall s \in \mathrm{sources},\; \mathrm{novelty}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = \mathrm{false} \lor \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{this\_deposit\_claims\_no\_novelty\_today}]
\[ \left|\{\, x \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{novelty}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_restated\_sources\_are\_named\_and\_claim\_nothing}]
\[ \forall s \in \{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 0 \,\},\; \mathrm{novelty}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_unsearched\_source\_claims\_nothing}]
\[ \forall s \in \{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 1 \,\},\; \mathrm{novelty}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_source\_is\_classified}]
\[ \forall s \in \mathrm{sources},\; \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 0 \lor \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 1 \lor \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_kinds\_cover\_every\_source}]
\[ \left|\{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 0 \,\}\right| + \left|\{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 1 \,\}\right| + \left|\{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 2 \,\}\right| = \left|\mathrm{sources}\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{some\_sources\_are\_unsearched}]
\[ \left|\{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 1 \,\}\right| > 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{zero\_claims\_is\_not\_full\_attribution}]
\[ \left|\{\, x \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{novelty}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| = 0 \land \left|\{\, s \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{kindOf}\mathopen{}\left(s\right) = 1 \,\}\right| > 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{novelty\_is\_claimed\_of\_no\_source}]
\[ \left|\{\, x \in \mathrm{sources} \mid \mathrm{novelty}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{verdict.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_input\_receives\_one\_of\_the\_five\_verdicts}]
\[ \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(k \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(r \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(s \mapsto \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) \le 4\right)\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_five\_classes\_do\_not\_overlap}]
\[ \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(k \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(r \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(s \mapsto \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 0 = k < 2 \land \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 1 = k \ge 2 \land r > 0 \land \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 4 = k \ge 2 \land r = 0 \land s \ge \mathrm{sources} \land \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 3 = k \ge 2 \land r = 0 \land s > 0 \land s < \mathrm{sources} \land \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 2 = k \ge 2 \land r = 0 \land s = 0\right)\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_source\_going\_silent\_never\_cleans\_the\_verdict}]
\[ \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(k \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(r \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(s \mapsto \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 1 \lor \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s + 1\right) \ge \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right)\right)\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_clean\_verdict\_is\_reachable\_only\_from\_complete\_evidence}]
\[ \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(k \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(r \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(s \mapsto \lnot \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 2 \lor s = 0 \land r = 0 \land k \ge 2\right)\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_relevant\_hit\_outranks\_any\_amount\_of\_silence}]
\[ \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(k \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(r \mapsto \mathrm{R.all}\mathopen{}\left(s \mapsto \lnot k \ge 2 \land r > 0 \lor \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right) = 1\right)\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{all\_five\_verdicts\_occur}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{R.flatMap}\mathopen{}\left(k \mapsto \mathrm{R.flatMap}\mathopen{}\left(r \mapsto \mathrm{R.map}\mathopen{}\left(s \mapsto \mathrm{verdict}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,r,\,s\right)\right)\right)\right)\right)\right| = 5 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{too\_few\_terms\_precedes\_every\_other\_test}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_residue\_has\_an\_additive\_inverse}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{negation\_is\_an\_involution\_with\_zero\_its\_own\_inverse}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mod\_three\_classes\_partition\_the\_ring\_into\_three\_threes}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_two\_tetrahedra\_meet\_the\_cube\_at\_its\_own\_counts}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_triad\_lands\_on\_three\_hues\_a\_third\_of\_the\_circle\_apart}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mover\_loses\_exactly\_at\_the\_multiples\_of\_four}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{no\_hue\_on\_an\_odd\_wheel\_has\_an\_exact\_complement}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_inverse\_map\_applied\_twice\_is\_the\_identity\_on\_the\_units}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pascal\_is\_odd\_exactly\_where\_the\_index\_is\_a\_submask}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_live\_cells\_of\_row\_n\_number\_two\_to\_the\_popcount}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_beat\_relation\_is\_the\_successor\_in\_the\_trinity}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,2\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,2\},\; \mathrm{beats}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = a = b + 1 \bmod 3 \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,2\},\; \lnot \mathrm{beats}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,a\right) \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,2\},\; \left|\{\, b \in \{0,\dots,2\} \mid \mathrm{beats}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \,\}\right| = 1 \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,2\},\; a + 3 \bmod 3 = a \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_digit\_and\_its\_balance\_close\_both\_the\_ring\_and\_the\_circle}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_doubling\_orbit\_is\_the\_unit\_group\_and\_two\_generates\_it}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_sum\_collapse\_and\_the\_residue\_are\_the\_same\_map}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_step\_is\_the\_circle\_divided\_by\_the\_base\_and\_nine\_steps\_close\_it}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_are\_exactly\_the\_residues\_coprime\_to\_the\_base}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{gcd9}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = 1 \,\} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \mathrm{units} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{gcd9}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \neq 1 \,\} = [3,\,6,\,9] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8,748 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_triad\_is\_the\_multiples\_of\_three\_and\_the\_nilpotents}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid d \bmod 3 = 0 \,\} = [3,\,6,\,9] \land \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \lnot d \in \mathrm{units} \,\} = [3,\,6,\,9] \land \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \exists k \in \{1,\dots,4\} : \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(d^{k}\right) = 0 \,\} = [3,\,6,\,9] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 78,732 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unit\_group\_sums\_to\_zero\_and\_its\_product\_is\_the\_reflection\_of\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(\sum \mathrm{units}\right) = 0 \land \sum \mathrm{units} = 27 \land \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(\operatorname{fold}_{(\cdot\,\cdot\,\cdot)}\left(\mathrm{units},\, 1\right)\right) = 8 \land \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(8 + 1\right) = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_step\_base\_factors\_as\_two\_to\_the\_fourth\_times\_three\_cubed}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_tens\_complement\_fixes\_exactly\_the\_centre}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,10\} \mid \mathrm{refl10}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \,\} = [5] \land \forall d \in \{1,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{refl10}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl10}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \land \forall d \in \{1,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{refl10}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) + d = 10 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_balancing\_complement\_permutes\_the\_units}]
\[ \{\, 9 - d \mid d \in \mathrm{units} \,\} = [8,\,7,\,5,\,4,\,2,\,1] \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, 9 - d \mid d \in \mathrm{units} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall d \in \mathrm{units},\; 9 - d \in \mathrm{units} \land \forall d \in \mathrm{units},\; 9 - 9 - d = d \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_cube\_mod\_nine\_is\_zero\_one\_or\_eight}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(d^{3}\right) \in [0,\,1,\,8] \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(d^{3}\right) \mid d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \,\}\right)\right| = 3 \land \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(d^{3}\right) = 0 \,\} = [0,\,3,\,6] \land \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(d^{3}\right) = 1 \,\} = [1,\,4,\,7] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 177,147 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{doubling\_is\_multiplication\_by\_two\_and\_the\_reduction\_commutes}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot d\right) = \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \cdot 2\right) \land \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; 2 \cdot d = d + d \land \forall k \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2^{k + 1}\right) = \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot 2^{k}\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 486 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_primitive\_roots\_are\_exactly\_two\_and\_five}]
\[ \{\, u \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{powersOf}\mathopen{}\left(u\right)\right)\right| = 6 \,\} = [2,\,5] \land \mathrm{phi}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) = 6 \land \mathrm{phi}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = 2 \land \left|\{\, u \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{powersOf}\mathopen{}\left(u\right)\right)\right| = 6 \,\}\right| = \mathrm{phi}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{phi}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 162 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_unit\_is\_a\_power\_of\_two\_for\_exactly\_one\_exponent}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{units},\; \exists k \in \{0,\dots,5\} : \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2^{k}\right) = u \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2^{k}\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall u \in \mathrm{units},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2^{\mathrm{ind}\mathopen{}\left(u\right)}\right) = u \land \{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2^{k}\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,8,\,7,\,5] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,296 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_index\_carries\_multiplication\_to\_addition\_mod\_six}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{units},\; \forall v \in \mathrm{units},\; \mathrm{ind}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(u \cdot v\right)\right) = \mathrm{ind}\mathopen{}\left(u\right) + \mathrm{ind}\mathopen{}\left(v\right) \bmod 6 \land \mathrm{ind}\mathopen{}\left(1\right) = 0 \land \left|\mathrm{units}\right| = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{no\_even\_position\_ever\_survives}]
