\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{in\_a\_magic\_square\_the\_centre\_is\_five\_and\_the\_corners\_are\_even}]
\[ \{\, c \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid c + c = 10 \,\} = [5] \land \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid d \bmod 2 = 0 \,\} = [2,\,4,\,6,\,8] \land \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid d \bmod 2 = 1 \,\} = [1,\,3,\,5,\,7,\,9] \land \forall d \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; d + 10 - d = 10 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 131,220 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pisano\_period\_mod\_nine\_is\_twenty\_four\_and\_four\_times\_the\_doubling\_order}]
\texttt{((List.range' 1 30).filter (fun k => fibPair k == (0, 1))).getD 0 0 = 24 ∧ 24 = 4 * 6 ∧ fibPair 24 = (0, 1) ∧ (List.range' 1 23).all (fun k => fibPair k != (0, 1))}

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 690 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{each\_stern\_row\_sums\_to\_a\_power\_of\_three}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,7\},\; \sum \mathrm{stern}[\{2^{k},\dots,2^{k}+2^{k}-1\}] = 3^{k} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_euclid\_triple\_is\_primitive\_exactly\_when\_its\_seeds\_are\_coprime\_and\_opposite}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,12\},\; \forall n \in \{1,\dots,1+m - 1-1\},\; \mathrm{gcdOf}\mathopen{}\left(m \cdot m - n \cdot n,\,2 \cdot m \cdot n\right) = 1 = \mathrm{gcdOf}\mathopen{}\left(m,\,n\right) = 1 \land m + n \bmod 2 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_product\_of\_two\_sums\_of\_squares\_is\_a\_sum\_of\_squares}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \forall d \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; a \cdot a + b \cdot b \cdot c \cdot c + d \cdot d = a \cdot c - b \cdot d \cdot a \cdot c - b \cdot d + a \cdot d + b \cdot c \cdot a \cdot d + b \cdot c \lor a \cdot c < b \cdot d \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 2,401 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{minus\_one\_is\_a\_square\_mod\_p\_exactly\_when\_p\_is\_one\_mod\_four}]
\[ \forall p \in \{\, x \in \{3,\dots,49\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; \mathrm{isQR}\mathopen{}\left(p - 1,\,p\right) = p \bmod 4 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 47 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{two\_is\_a\_square\_mod\_p\_exactly\_when\_p\_is\_one\_or\_seven\_mod\_eight}]
\[ \forall p \in \{\, x \in \{3,\dots,49\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; \mathrm{isQR}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,p\right) = p \bmod 8 = 1 \lor p \bmod 8 = 7 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 47 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reciprocity\_holds\_for\_every\_pair\_of\_odd\_primes\_below\_thirty}]
\[ \forall p \in \{\, x \in \{3,\dots,29\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; \forall q \in \{\, x \in \{3,\dots,29\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; p = q \lor \mathrm{isQR}\mathopen{}\left(q,\,p\right) = \mathrm{isQR}\mathopen{}\left(p,\,q\right) = \lnot p \bmod 4 = 3 \land q \bmod 4 = 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nine\_point\_wheel\_the\_dashboard\_draws\_closes\_the\_circle}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{hue}[\{1,\dots,9\}]\right| = 9 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{hue}[\{1,\dots,9\}]\right)\right| = 9 \land \mathrm{hue}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) = 0 \land 8 \cdot 40 + 40 = 360 \land \forall d \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{hue}\mathopen{}\left(d + 1\right) + 360 - \mathrm{hue}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \bmod 360 = 40 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 648 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_plus\_one\_distinguished\_element\_number\_seven}]
\texttt{units.length = 6 ∧ units.length + 1 = 7 ∧ (units.contains 0) = false ∧ (0 :: units).length = 7 ∧ ((0 :: units).eraseDups).length = 7}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digital\_root\_carries\_both\_sum\_and\_product}]
\[ \forall a \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(a + b\right) = \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(a\right) + \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(b\right)\right) \land \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot b\right) = \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(a\right) \cdot \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(b\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,600 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_six\_units\_fall\_into\_three\_pairs\_that\_sum\_to\_nine}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{units},\; 9 - u \in \mathrm{units} \land \forall u \in \mathrm{units},\; 9 - 9 - u = u \land \forall u \in \mathrm{units},\; 9 - u \neq u \land \frac{\left|\mathrm{units}\right|}{2} = 3 \land \{\, u + 9 - u \mid u \in \{\, u \in \mathrm{units} \mid u < 9 - u \,\} \,\} = [9,\,9,\,9] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 3 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_generates\_the\_units\_mod\_seven\_and\_they\_number\_six}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{powers7}\mathopen{}\left(3\right)\right)\right| = 6 \land \{\, u \in \{1,\dots,6\} \mid \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{powers7}\mathopen{}\left(u\right)\right)\right| = 6 \,\} = [3,\,5] \land \left|\{1,\dots,6\}\right| = 6 \land 6 + 1 = 7 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 72 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_two\_unit\_groups\_are\_cyclic\_of\_order\_six\_and\_correspond\_by\_index}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{units}\right| = 6 \land \left|\{1,\dots,6\}\right| = 6 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2^{k}\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, 3^{k} \bmod 7 \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 216 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nines\_check\_fires\_exactly\_when\_the\_error\_is\_not\_a\_multiple\_of\_nine}]
\[ \forall v \in \{1,\dots,60\},\; \forall e \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(v + e\right) \neq \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(v\right) = e \bmod 9 \neq 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,800 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nines\_check\_cannot\_see\_a\_transposition\_and\_the\_elevens\_check\_can}]
\[ \forall n \in \{10,\dots,98\},\; \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{root}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{swap2}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) \land \exists n \in \{10,\dots,98\} : n \bmod 11 \neq \mathrm{swap2}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 11 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7,921 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{five\_is\_the\_reflection\_fixed\_point\_and\_the\_inverse\_of\_two}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,10\} \mid \mathrm{refl10}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \,\} = [5] \land \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot 5\right) = 1 \land \mathrm{inv}\mathopen{}\left(2\right) = 5 \land \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot d\right) = 1 \,\} = [5] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 90 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nonzero\_residues\_vanish\_exactly\_for\_an\_odd\_modulus}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,31\},\; \sum \{1,\dots,1+n - 1-1\} \bmod n = 0 = n \bmod 2 = 1 \land \forall n \in \{2,\dots,31\},\; \sum \{1,\dots,1+n - 1-1\} = \frac{n \cdot n - 1}{2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 900 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unit\_multiplication\_table\_is\_a\_latin\_square}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{units},\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot b\right) \mid b \in \mathrm{units} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall b \in \mathrm{units},\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot b\right) \mid a \in \mathrm{units} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall a \in \mathrm{units},\; \forall b \in \mathrm{units},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot b\right) \in \mathrm{units} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_additive\_generators\_are\_exactly\_the\_multiplicative\_units}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{addSpan}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right)\right| = 9 \,\} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \land \mathrm{units} = [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 324 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_axis\_is\_closed\_under\_addition\_and\_under\_multiplication\_by\_the\_ring}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{axis3},\; \forall b \in \mathrm{axis3},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(a + b\right) \in \mathrm{axis3} \land \forall a \in \mathrm{axis3},\; \forall r \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot r\right) \in \mathrm{axis3} \land \left|\mathrm{axis3}\right| = 3 \land \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,8\} \mid \lnot d \in \mathrm{units} \,\} = [0,\,3,\,6] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 243 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_ring\_of\_residues\_is\_a\_field\_exactly\_at\_a\_prime\_modulus}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,21\},\; \forall d \in \{1,\dots,1+n - 1-1\},\; \exists e \in \{0,\dots,n-1\} : d \cdot e \bmod n = 1 = \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \land \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{squaring\_maps\_the\_six\_units\_onto\_a\_closed\_set\_of\_three}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(u \cdot u\right) \mid u \in \mathrm{units} \,\}\right)\right| = 3 \land \operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(u \cdot u\right) \mid u \in \mathrm{units} \,\}\right) = [1,\,4,\,7] \land \forall a \in [1,\,4,\,7],\; \forall b \in [1,\,4,\,7],\; \mathrm{m9}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot b\right) \in [1,\,4,\,7] \land \frac{\left|\mathrm{units}\right|}{3} = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 81 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{five\_six\_one\_is\_composite\_and\_fools\_fermat\_for\_every\_base}]
\[ 561 = 3 \cdot 11 \cdot 17 \land \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(561\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \mathrm{fermatFools}\mathopen{}\left(561\right) \land \mathrm{fermatFools}\mathopen{}\left(341\right) = \mathrm{false} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassinis\_identity\_alternates\_by\_one\_either\_side\_of\_the\_square}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \begin{cases}\mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) + 1 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{each\_binomial\_row\_sums\_to\_a\_power\_of\_two\_and\_splits\_evenly}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,12\},\; \sum \mathrm{binom}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)[\{0,\dots,n + 1-1\}] = 2^{n} \land \forall n \in \{1,\dots,12\},\; \sum \mathrm{binom}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)[\{\, k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \mid k \bmod 2 = 0 \,\}] = \sum \mathrm{binom}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)[\{\, k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \mid k \bmod 2 = 1 \,\}] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 156 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_to\_the\_p\_returns\_a\_modulo\_every\_prime\_in\_range}]
\[ \forall p \in \{\, x \in \{2,\dots,29\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; \forall a \in \{0,\dots,p-1\},\; a^{p} \bmod p = a \bmod p \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 28 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_start\_below\_a\_thousand\_reaches\_one\_within\_two\_hundred\_steps}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,999\},\; \mathrm{collatzSteps}\mathopen{}\left(200,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 999 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_number\_of\_divisors\_is\_odd\_exactly\_for\_a\_perfect\_square}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,80\},\; \mathrm{divisorCount}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 2 = 1 = \exists r \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} : r \cdot r = n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 80 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{euclids\_formula\_always\_produces\_a\_right\_triangle}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,15\},\; \forall n \in \{1,\dots,1+m - 1-1\},\; m \cdot m - n \cdot n \cdot m \cdot m - n \cdot n + 2 \cdot m \cdot n \cdot 2 \cdot m \cdot n = m \cdot m + n \cdot n \cdot m \cdot m + n \cdot n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 14 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reduction\_to\_coprime\_moduli\_is\_a\_bijection\_over\_one\_period}]
\texttt{[(2, 3), (3, 4), (4, 9), (5, 7), (8, 9)].all (fun pr => let m1 := pr.1; let m2 := pr.2 (((List.range (m1 * m2)).map (fun x => (x \% m1, x \% m2))).eraseDups).length == m1 * m2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_derangements\_follow\_the\_subfactorial\_recurrence}]
\[ \mathrm{derange}[\{0,\dots,9\}] = [1,\,0,\,1,\,2,\,9,\,44,\,265,\,1854,\,14833,\,133496] \land \forall n \in \{2,\dots,9\},\; \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = n - 1 \cdot \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 800 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_lucas\_square\_sits\_four\_either\_side\_of\_five\_fibonacci\_squares}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,18\},\; \begin{cases}\mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = 5 \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 4 & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 4 = 5 \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \land \forall n \in \{1,\dots,18\},\; \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 324 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_sum\_agrees\_with\_the\_value\_modulo\_three\_and\_modulo\_nine}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,300\},\; \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 3 = n \bmod 3 \land \forall n \in \{1,\dots,300\},\; \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 9 = n \bmod 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 90,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_partitions\_obey\_eulers\_pentagonal\_recurrence}]
\[ \forall n \in \{7,\dots,11\},\; p\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + p\mathopen{}\left(n - 5\right) + p\mathopen{}\left(n - 7\right) = p\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + p\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \land \forall g \in [1,\,2,\,5,\,7],\; \exists k \in \{1,\dots,3\} : \frac{k \cdot 3 \cdot k - 1}{2} = g \lor \frac{k \cdot 3 \cdot k + 1}{2} = g \land p\mathopen{}\left(11\right) = 56 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_derangements\_are\_n\_times\_the\_previous\_with\_one\_alternating\_correction}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,7\},\; \begin{cases}n \cdot \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + 1 = \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ n \cdot \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) = \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_n\_fibonacci\_numbers\_sum\_to\_the\_term\_two\_further\_on\_less\_one}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,12\},\; \sum \mathrm{fib}[\{1,\dots,1+n-1\}] + 1 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 2\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_n\_naturals\_squares\_and\_cubes\_have\_their\_closed\_forms}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \frac{n \cdot n + 1}{2} \land \forall n \in \{1,\dots,15\},\; \sum \{\, k \cdot k \mid k \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \,\} = \frac{n \cdot n + 1 \cdot 2 \cdot n + 1}{6} \land \forall n \in \{1,\dots,12\},\; \sum \{\, k \cdot k \cdot k \mid k \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \,\} = \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 3,600 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{eight\_triangular\_numbers\_plus\_one\_is\_the\_odd\_square}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,29\},\; 8 \cdot \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 = 2 \cdot n + 1 \cdot 2 \cdot n + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_josephus\_survivor\_is\_twice\_the\_remainder\_above\_a\_power\_of\_two\_plus\_one}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{jos}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = 2 \cdot n - \mathrm{largestPow2}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 \land \forall n \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{largestPow2}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \le n \land 2 \cdot \mathrm{largestPow2}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) > n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,600 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_euclid\_perfect\_number\_is\_a\_triangular\_number}]
\[ \forall q \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7],\; \mathrm{perfect}\mathopen{}\left(q\right) = \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(2^{q} - 1\right) \land \mathrm{perfect}\mathopen{}\left(2\right) = \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(3\right) \land \mathrm{perfect}\mathopen{}\left(3\right) = \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(7\right) \land \mathrm{perfect}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = \mathrm{sumTo}\mathopen{}\left(31\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4 cases. \hfill$\square$

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{qr\_u1}]
\[ \exists d \in \{0,\dots,8\} : d \cdot d \bmod 9 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{qr\_u4}]
\[ \exists d \in \{0,\dots,8\} : d \cdot d \bmod 9 = 4 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$
